## 1. CCM 核心进展（2）

### A（2）

#### [A] YAP Fuels Cerebral Cavernous Malformations by Regulating KLF4 Transcriptional Activity and Protein Stability.

- PMID: 42921278
- Domain: CCM
- Domain 依据: Strong CCM disease/gene expression in title or abstract
- 转化相关性: Direct
- 转化相关性依据: The article has strong direct CCM evidence
- 优先级依据: A: CCM core omics/mechanism
- 主题: CM基础机制
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: MedComm
- 作者: Ye T, Zheng Q, Hu Z, Fan H, Sun Z, Li X, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1002/mco2.71086
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42921278/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-26

#### 摘要

Cerebral cavernous malformations (CCMs), which frequently predispose patients to strokes or seizures, primarily arise from endothelial specific loss-of-function mutations in the CCM genes, and occasionally accompanied by gain-of-function mutations in PIK3CA and MAP3K3. While the Hippo pathway is a well-established regulator of tissue homeostasis, its emerging relevance in vascular pathology particularly its precise role in CCM pathogenesis remains poorly understood. In this study, we demonstrate that CCM gene deficiency suppresses endothelial Hippo signaling, as evidenced by diminished LATS activity, alongside concomitant elevation of YAP expression and nuclear translocation in both human and mouse CCM lesions. Notably, endothelial-specific deletion of Yap mitigates CCM lesion formation, reinforces endothelial junctions, enhances inflammatory resistance, and decreases KLF4 protein levels in a CCM mouse model. Conversely, intracranial Adeno-associated virus (AAV)-mediated inhibition of LATS or activation of YAP promoted CCMs formation under Ccm2 deficiency. Mechanistically, under the dual coordination of negative regulation by LATS and positive regulation by MAP3K3, YAP orchestrates KLF4 transcriptional activity and protein stability. This is achieved through a direct interaction with KLF4 that shields it from β-TrCP-mediated proteasomal degradation, thereby driving CCM progression. Collectively, our findings unveil a novel dimension to Hippo pathway regulation and underscore YAP as a pivotal driver of CCM pathogenesis.

#### [A] kri-1/KRIT1 promotes innate immune and lipid homeostasis in the C. elegans intestine.

- PMID: 42961997
- Domain: CCM
- Domain 依据: Strong CCM disease/gene expression in title or abstract
- 转化相关性: Direct
- 转化相关性依据: The article has strong direct CCM evidence
- 优先级依据: A: CCM core omics/mechanism
- 主题: CM基础机制
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: PLoS genetics
- 作者: Alaghatta A, Tse-Kang SY, Bollen D, Wani KA, Peterson ND, Pukkila-Worley R
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 30
- DOI: 10.1371/journal.pgen.1012166
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42961997/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Animals must differentially allocate essential metabolic resources in response to changing environmental conditions to ensure reproductive success. For example, adult C. elegans coordinate innate immune defenses during infection and also allocate fat stores to the germline, each of which are required for evolutionary fitness. The genetic mechanisms that integrate resource allocation and host defenses in this context, however, are not fully understood. From a forward genetic screen for novel regulators of innate immune gene transcription, we identified kri-1, the nematode homolog of human Krev interaction trapped protein KRIT1. Mutations in human KRIT1 underlie cerebral cavernous malformations, a disease marked by defects in vascular integrity. Similarly, C. elegans kri-1/KRIT1 is required for the integrity of the intestinal epithelial cell barrier. We find also that kri-1/KRIT1 is required for host tolerance to bacterial infection, fecundity, and normal development. In this context, kri-1/KRIT1 restrains the activity of the cytoprotective transcription factor skn-1/NRF2 to control immune gene transcription and likely intestinal lipid mobilization during aging, a process necessary for healthy reproduction, but functions independently of skn-1/NRF2 to promote epithelial integrity and pathogen tolerance. These data reveal a broad role for a strongly conserved regulator in essential physiological processes that are required for reproductive fidelity and evolutionary success.

## 2. Related vascular malformations（31）

### Venous malformation（9）

#### [A] Laparoscopic and endoscopic-assisted resection of small bowel venous malformations in blue rubber bleb nevus syndrome (BRBNS): a technical case report with operative video.

- PMID: 42931919
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Surgical endoscopy
- 作者: Boyle MA, Anand JK, Zacur GM, Church JT
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 26
- DOI: 10.1007/s00464-026-13522-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42931919/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Blue Rubber Bleb Nevus Syndrome (BRBNS) is a rare condition characterized by venous malformations in multiple organ systems, most commonly presenting with cutaneous lesions in early childhood followed by complications from gastrointestinal bleeding in adolescence and early adulthood. Once symptomatic, this condition is often managed with endoscopic techniques and medical therapies that inhibit angiogenesis. However, patients may require surgical management when endoscopic approaches are not feasible, or if patients continue to have persistent gastrointestinal bleeding after maximizing endoscopic and medical therapy. Although surgery can achieve complete resection of gastrointestinal venous malformations, determining the optimal operative approach may be challenging. Lesions can vary substantially in size, depth, location, and extent of bowel involvement. As a result, some patients may require highly invasive procedures, including resections associated with substantial loss of bowel length. Given the early age at presentation and the potential need for future interventions, careful documentation of preoperative evaluation and minimally invasive approaches for managing gastrointestinal lesions associated with BRBNS is essential. In this technical case report, we describe a combined minimally invasive laparoscopic and intraoperative endoscopic approach for the treatment of small bowel venous malformations associated with BRBNS in an adolescent patient. Although feasible in this case, BRBNS remains a rare and heterogeneous condition that may still require invasive surgical management. Further studies are needed to better define the natural history of this condition and long-term outcomes, particularly in the absence of a curative treatment and the early age at which many patients are affected.

#### [A] Effectiveness of sclerotherapy, surgery, and laser therapy in patients with venous malformations: a systematic review.

- PMID: 24196269
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Cardiovascular and interventional radiology
- 作者: van der Vleuten CJ, Kater A, Wijnen MH, Schultze Kool LJ, Rovers MM
- 发表日期: 2014 Aug
- DOI: 10.1007/s00270-013-0764-2
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24196269/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: Because the best possible treatment for venous malformations is unclear, this study systematically reviews the available literature regarding the effectiveness of different treatment options for the patient group. Venous malformations result from incorrect development of the veins during embryogenesis and are present at birth. Venous malformations may exhibit symptoms, such as pain, swelling, and inflammation of the vessel.
MATERIALS AND METHODS: A systematic literature search in PubMed and Embase was performed. Data regarding the design, participants, intervention and, treatment outcome (success and complications) were extracted. The validity of the studies was assessed with the Cochrane Collaboration's risk of bias tool.
RESULTS: Thirty-five studies were identified studying the effectiveness of eight treatments: sclerotherapy/embolization with ethanol, gelified ethanol, bleomycin, polidocanol, sodium tetradecyl sulfate (STS), Ethibloc, surgery, and laser therapy. All of the included studies have a high or unclear risk of bias. The average biased reported success rates for ethanol, gelified ethanol, bleomycin, polidocanol, STS, Ethibloc, surgery, and laser therapy were 74, 89, 88, 90, 86, 65, 90, and 94 %, respectively.
CONCLUSION: Until more valid evidence is available, the choice for treatment remains a shared decision between the patient and a multidisciplinary treatment group. From a cost perspective, sclerotherapy with STS or polidocanol should be the treatment of choice.

#### [B] Venous malformations.

- PMID: 25241098
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Seminars in pediatric surgery
- 作者: Dasgupta R, Patel M
- 发表日期: 2014 Aug
- DOI: 10.1053/j.sempedsurg.2014.06.019
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25241098/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Venous malformations are slow-flow congenital malformations. They consist of abnormal venous channels that do not involute. Venous malformations can either be superficial or involve deeper structures. Patients with venous malformations are often symptomatic. The most common complaint is pain from congestion, mass effect, or compression of neural structures. Swelling, compromise of function, and disfigurement are other common symptoms. Large lesions cause coagulopathy. Therapeutic options for the treatment of these patients include sclerotherapy, compression garments, and surgical resection. These complex patients are best treated in a multi-disciplinary clinic environment, as they require long-term follow-up throughout childhood into adulthood.

#### [B] Endoscopic therapy for gastrointestinal bleeding in Klippel-Trénaunay syndrome: a case report and literature review.

- PMID: 42959170
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Frontiers in medicine
- 作者: Zhang R, Shi W, Wu X, Yang A, Chen Y
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fmed.2026.1960274
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959170/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Klippel-Trénaunay syndrome (KTS) is a rare congenital vascular anomaly characterized by capillary malformation, underlying low-flow venous/lymphatic malformations, and bone- and/or soft-tissue limb hypertrophy of the affected extremity. At present, there is no consensus on the optimal management of KTS, particularly in the context of gastrointestinal bleeding.
CASE PRESENTATION: We describe a case of gastrointestinal bleeding in a patient with KTS who presented with colorectal venous malformations with phleboliths, multiple splenic vascular malformations, penile varicosities, and hypertrophy of the right lower extremity and the right buttock. A colonoscopy revealed a circumferential mucosal elevation with bluish discoloration extending from 15 cm above the anal verge in the colorectum, and active bleeding was observed at 12 cm from the anal verge. Endoscopic submucosal sclerotherapy with 1-1.5 mL of lauromacrogol (1%) per site and a total volume of 30 mL, combined with band ligation using six bands, was performed. This approach has rarely been reported and resulted in significant relief from bleeding. At the 6-month follow-up, the patient's symptoms and hemoglobin levels remained stable, with no other complications.
CONCLUSION: Although KTS remains incurable, early endoscopic recognition facilitates diagnosis, and endoscopic therapy may serve as an important treatment modality for gastrointestinal involvement.

#### [B] Intralesional copper wire retention and pingyangmycin injection: an effective combinational therapy for complex venous malformation in soft tissue.

- PMID: 23508003
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Phlebology
- 作者: Yuan SM, Hong ZJ, Jiang HQ, Wang J, Hu XB
- 发表日期: 2014 Apr
- DOI: 10.1177/0268355513477086
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/23508003/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVES: Complex venous malformations (VMs) may extensively involve the soft tissue. The treatment remains a challenge till now. Here we introduce a combinational therapy of copper wires and pingyangmycin (bleomycin A5,PYM).
METHODS: Copper wires were retained in VMs by repeated penetration with a straight needle. Subsequently, PYM solution was injected into the lesion. Eight to 10 days later, copper wires were removed. The dressing was changed every day until the puncture pores healed. Magnetic resonance imaging scanning was performed to observe the change of VMs.
RESULTS: From January 2001 to December 2011, 56 patients were treated. During the follow-up period, most of the VMs shrunk obviously. The symptoms were relieved or disappeared. The complications included local pain, temporary paraesthesia and moderate fever, which disappeared quickly after the removal of copper wires.
CONCLUSIONS: This combinational therapy is a safe and effective approach for the complex VMs in soft tissue.

#### [C] European guidelines for sclerotherapy in chronic venous disorders.

- PMID: 23559590
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: C: broad, exploratory, low-relevance, or unmatched article
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Phlebology
- 作者: Rabe E, Breu FX, Cavezzi A, Coleridge Smith P, Frullini A, Gillet JL, et al.
- 发表日期: 2014 Jul
- DOI: 10.1177/0268355513483280
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/23559590/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

AIM: Sclerotherapy is the targeted chemical ablation of varicose veins by intravenous injection of a liquid or foamed sclerosing drug. The treated veins may be intradermal, subcutaneous, and/or transfascial as well as superficial and deep in venous malformations. The aim of this guideline is to give evidence-based recommendations for liquid and foam sclerotherapy.
METHODS: This guideline was drafted on behalf of 23 European Phlebological Societies during a Guideline Conference on 7-10 May 2012 in Mainz. The conference was organized by the German Society of Phlebology. These guidelines review the present state of knowledge as reflected in published medical literature. The regulatory situation of sclerosant drugs differs from country to country but this has not been considered in this document. The recommendations of this guideline are graded according to the American College of Chest Physicians Task Force recommendations on Grading Strength of Recommendations and Quality of Evidence in Clinical Guidelines.
RESULTS: This guideline focuses on the two sclerosing drugs which are licensed in the majority of the European countries, polidocanol and sodium tetradecyl sulphate. Other sclerosants are not discussed in detail. The guideline gives recommendations concerning indications, contraindications, side-effects, concentrations, volumes, technique and efficacy of liquid and foam sclerotherapy of varicose veins and venous malformations.

#### [C] Sclerotherapy of varicose veins in dermatology.

- PMID: 24797742
- Domain: Venous malformation
- Domain 依据: Venous or slow-flow malformation expression in article content

- DOI: 10.1111/ddg.12333
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24797742/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

Venous disorders rank among the most frequent diseases in the German population. Early diagnostic investigation and treatment can prevent their progression and may reduce the risk for secondary diseases. The therapeutic spectrum for varicose veins includes conservative as well as interventional and surgical methods. Because it is minimally invasive and well-tolerated, sclerotherapy represents an important treatment method for venous insufficiency, recurrent varicosis and venous malformations. We review the role of sclerotherapy as a treatment option of chronic venous insufficiency in dermatology.

### Brain AVM / AVM（9）

#### [A] Arteriovenous Malformations as Neoplastic-Like Lesions: Molecular Underpinnings and Implications for the Interventional Radiologist.

- PMID: 42934295
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形经典文献/综述补位
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Cardiovascular and interventional radiology
- 作者: Shashi KK, Gonzalez Garcia A, Sabado J, Richter GT, Tran AD, Chewning R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 26
- DOI: 10.1007/s00270-026-04650-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42934295/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-29

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) are high-flow vascular anomalies traditionally treated as congenital structural lesions with embolization or surgical resection. However, recurrence after embolization alone exceeds 90%, underscoring incomplete understanding of their biology. Next-generation sequencing has identified somatic gain-of-function mutations in KRAS, MAP2K1, BRAF, and HRAS as drivers of sporadic AVMs. Confined to lesional endothelial cells, these mutations activate RAS/MAPK signaling and support a neoplastic-like rather than purely structural disease model. This has therapeutic implications: MEK inhibitors, particularly trametinib, have shown angiographic and clinical responses in refractory AVMs. Interventional radiologists can obtain draining vein liquid-biopsy samples during embolization for molecular genotyping; embolization may increase circulating cell-free DNA yield. This review summarizes the molecular rationale, targeted therapy evidence, and integration of genotyping into embolization workflows to enable combined treatment strategies.Level of Evidence: No level of evidence assigned (Review Article).

#### [B] What is the best therapeutic approach to a pediatric patient with a deep-seated brain AVM?

- PMID: 30980204
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Neurosurgical review
- 作者: Meling TR, Patet G
- 发表日期: 2019 Jun
- DOI: 10.1007/s10143-019-01101-8
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30980204/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-28

#### 摘要

Although brain arteriovenous malformations (bAVMs) account for a very small proportion of cerebral pathologies in the pediatric population, they are the cause of roughly 50% of spontaneous intracranial hemorrhages. Pediatric bAVMs tend to rupture more frequently and seem to have higher recurrence rates than bAVMs in adults. Thus, the management of pediatric bAVMs is particularly challenging. In general, the treatment options are conservative treatment, microsurgery, endovascular therapy (EVT), gamma knife radiosurgery (GKRS), proton-beam stereotactic radiosurgery (PSRS), or a combination of the above. In order to identify the best approach to deep-seated pediatric bAVMs, we performed a systematic review, according to the PRISMA guidelines. None of the options seem to offer a clear advantage over the others when used alone. Microsurgery provides the highest obliteration rate, but has higher incidence of neurological complications. EVT may play a role when used as adjuvant therapy, but as a stand-alone therapy, the efficacy is low and the long-term side effects of radiation from the multiple sessions required in deep-seated pediatric bAVMs are still unknown. GKRS has a low risk of complication, but the obliteration rates still leave much to be desired. Finally, PSRS offers promising results with a more accurate radiation that avoids the surrounding tissue, but data is limited due to its recent introduction. Overall, a multi-modal approach, or even an active surveillance, might be the most suitable when facing deep-seated bAVM, considering the difficulty of their management and the high risk of complications in the pediatric population.

#### [B] Unruptured brain arteriovenous malformations and hydrocephalus: Case series and review of the literature.

- PMID: 30948310
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Journal of clinical neuroscience : official journal of the Neurosurgical Society of Australasia
- 作者: De Maria L, Brinjikji W, Lanzino G
- 发表日期: 2019 Jun
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jocn.2019.03.042
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30948310/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-28

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVE: Hydrocephalus is an uncommon presentation of unruptured brain arteriovenous malformations (AVMs). The goal of this case series and literature review is to present possible pathological mechanisms, management strategies and outcomes in patients with hydrocephalus due to unruptured AVMs.
METHODS: Three consecutive patients with hydrocephalus caused by unruptured AVMs as well as all cases previously reported in the literature were retrospectively reviewed to determine clinical symptoms, AVM location, nidus size, venous drainage, mechanism of hydrocephalus, level/cause of obstruction, and degree of hydrocephalus. Management of hydrocephalus, AVM treatment, and follow-up length were evaluated.
RESULTS: Of 350 patients unruptured AVMs, 3 presented with hydrocephalus (0.8%). In the literature review we found an additional 22 patients for a total of 25 cases. Eighteen patients had mechanical obstruction by the draining vein or the AVM nidus, usually at the level of the aqueduct (52%). Impaired cerebrospinal fluid resorption secondary to venous congestion led to hydrocephalus in 7 patients. Ten patients were treated for both the AVM and hydrocephalus, 13 patients underwent treatment of either hydrocephalus, or the AVM alone. Treatment of hydrocephalus, with or without associated treatment of the brain AVM, resulted in improvement of symptoms in 92% of patients. No rupture of the AVM was reported at follow-up.
CONCLUSIONS: The most common cause of hydrocephalus in unruptured brain AVMs is mechanical obstruction by the draining vein if it is located in a strategic position. Treatment of hydrocephalus alone or with associated treatment of the AVM is safe and effective.

#### [B] Volume-Staged CyberKnife Stereotactic Radiosurgery for Treatment of Drug-Resistant Epilepsy for a Spetzler-Martin Grade V Arteriovenous Malformation: A Case Report and Review of the Literature.

- DOI: 10.1016/j.wneu.2019.01.296
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30797935/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-28

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Evidence to support the use of stereotactic radiosurgery (SRS) in the treatment of epilepsy secondary to cerebral arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) is emerging. However, these studies have not clearly demonstrated the use of SRS in the treatment of drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE) in high Spetzler-Martin grade (IV-V) AVMs.
CASE DESCRIPTION: We present a 48-year-old woman with DRE secondary to a Spetzler-Martin grade V cerebral AVM. She was treated with volume-staged SRS (VS-SRS) and achieved near-complete resolution of her seizures with incomplete obliteration of the AVM. Six years after treatment, she has experienced no serious complications.
CONCLUSIONS: VS-SRS successfully controlled seizures (Engel Outcome Measure of 1A) in a patient with intractable, DRE secondary to a high-grade cerebral AVM.

#### [B] Ileal Arteriovenous Malformation after Laparoscopic Right Hemicolectomy Successfully Treated with Interventional Radiology: A Case Report.

- PMID: 42952653
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content

- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Journal of the anus, rectum and colon
- 作者: Yoshida T, Akiyama Y, Yamauchi M, Nagata J, Murakami Y, Shibao K, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.23922/jarc.2026-063
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42952653/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) are generally considered congenital vascular anomalies, whereas postoperative iatrogenic AVMs and arteriovenous fistulas (AVFs) are rare. In colorectal surgery, newly detected aneurysm-like mesenteric vascular lesions during postoperative surveillance may be difficult to distinguish from aneurysms, pseudoaneurysms, AVMs, and AVFs. A 75-year-old woman underwent laparoscopic right hemicolectomy for transverse colon cancer. Preoperative contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) showed no vascular malformation or aneurysmal lesion. Six months after surgery, surveillance CT revealed a 3.8 mm aneurysm-like lesion in a peripheral branch of the superior mesenteric artery near the anastomotic site. Although the patient was asymptomatic and initially managed conservatively, the lesion enlarged to 6.7 mm over two years. Because a pseudoaneurysm with a potential risk of rupture could not be excluded, angiographic evaluation and treatment were performed. Angiography demonstrated three abnormal vessels arising from a terminal ileal arterial branch, early venous drainage into the ileal vein, and an associated venous aneurysm. The lesion was clinically diagnosed as an ileal AVM, although acquired AVF remained an important differential diagnosis. Transcatheter embolization using n-butyl cyanoacrylate was successful. This case suggests that AVM or AVF should be considered in the differential diagnosis of aneurysm-like mesenteric vascular lesions detected after colorectal surgery.

#### [B] Beyond Hereditary Haemorrhagic Telangiectasia (HHT): Capillary Malformation-Arteriovenous Syndrome (CM-AVM).

- PMID: 42937150
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate

- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Irish medical journal
- 作者: Haydar I, Leahy P, O'Connor TM
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 22
- DOI: 未提供
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42937150/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

PRESENTATION: A woman in her forties, asymptomatic, was referred to the respiratory clinic with suspected HHT due to mucocutaneous telangiectasia involving her face, lips, and hands. Auscultation revealed an audible bruit over her left posterior chest wall.
DIAGNOSIS: MRI brain and spine showed no evidence of cerebral arteriovenous malformations. Genetic testing identified a heterozygous EPHB4 splice variant consistent with CM-AVM syndrome. It was negative for mutations associated with HHT.
TREATMENT: Red flags indicating pulmonary, intracranial or spinal haemorrhage were explained to the patient. She was linked in with the HHT clinic for routine follow-up and received genetic counselling.
DISCUSSION: CM-AVM is a rare autosomal dominant vascular malformation syndrome that can clinically mimic HHT but is genetically and phenotypically distinct. It should be considered in patients presenting with mucocutaneous vascular lesions. Genetic testing is essential to guide appropriate surveillance, management, and genetic counselling.

#### [B] DSA radiomics and clinical features for hemorrhagic risk assessment in bAVM embolization.

- PMID: 42934581
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 脑AVM/动静脉畸形
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: BMC medical imaging
- 作者: Zeng N, Li C, Zhu J, Wang Q, Wen Z
- 发表日期: 2026 Jul 29
- DOI: 10.1186/s12880-026-02587-7
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42934581/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Hemorrhagic complications (HCs) remain a major cause of morbidity in endovascular treatment (EVT) for brain arteriovenous malformation (bAVM), while reliable EVT-specific risk assessment remains challenging. This study aimed to develop and internally evaluate an exploratory model integrating digital subtraction angiography (DSA) radiomics and clinical features to assess the risk of EVT-related HCs.
METHODS: This retrospective study included 620 consecutive bAVM patients who underwent first-time EVT between 2011 and 2024. Radiomics features were extracted from anteroposterior and lateral DSA images of the nidus. Clinical-only, radiomics-only, and combined models were developed using four algorithms with stratified five-fold cross-validation on the training set and evaluated on the test set. Model interpretability was performed using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP).
RESULTS: HCs occurred in 100 patients (16.1%). On the test set, the XGBoost-based fusion model achieved the best overall performance (AUC 0.81), showing a modest improvement over the radiomics-only XGBoost model while outperforming all clinical-only models. Radiomics features contributed substantially to model performance, with clinical variables providing complementary information.
CONCLUSIONS: A model integrating DSA radiomics and clinical features provides an exploratory approach to assessing the risk of EVT-related HCs in patients with bAVM. These findings suggest the potential value of DSA-derived radiomics for individualized peri-procedural risk assessment, although external validation is required before clinical application.

#### [B] Percutaneous sclerotherapy for vascular malformations: a systematic review.

- PMID: 24509373
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Annals of vascular surgery
- 作者: Gurgacz S, Zamora L, Scott NA
- 发表日期: 2014 Jul
- DOI: 10.1016/j.avsg.2014.01.008
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24509373/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: This systematic review assessed the available published evidence on the safety and efficacy of percutaneous sclerotherapy for patients with congenital vascular malformations.
METHODS: A systematic search of various electronic bibliographic databases was conducted in May 2013. A grey literature search was also performed. Study selection, data extraction, and assessment of study quality were undertaken by one reviewer and checked by another reviewer.
RESULTS: One systematic review, one randomized controlled trial, one nonrandomized controlled trial, and 24 case series studies were included. However, deficiencies in reporting, small sample sizes, and marked inter-study heterogeneity precluded a definitive synthesis of the data. Ethanol sclerotherapy appeared to be potentially effective in treating venous malformations, with the majority of patients achieving some lesion regression. However, it is associated with a 16% risk of major complications, including deep tissue injury, deep vein thrombosis, and nerve injury. While there was limited evidence that sclerotherapy with OK-432 was an effective treatment for lymphatic malformations, evidence for the use of sclerotherapy in patients with arteriovenous malformations was unclear.
CONCLUSIONS: Very limited evidence from case series studies suggested that sclerotherapy with ethanol and OK-432, administered over multiple sessions, was effective in the treatment of venous and lymphatic malformations, respectively. However, the value of percutaneous sclerotherapy as a pretreatment for or an alternative to surgery is not known. Further evidence is required to delineate which patients will benefit most from percutaneous sclerotherapy to ensure that the advantages of treatment will outweigh the risks.

#### [C] EphrinB2-EphB4-RASA1 Signaling in Human Cerebrovascular Development and Disease.

- PMID: 30819650
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM

- DOI: 10.1016/j.molmed.2019.01.009
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30819650/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Recent whole exome sequencing studies in humans have provided novel insight into the importance of the ephrinB2-EphB4-RASA1 signaling axis in cerebrovascular development, corroborating and extending previous work in model systems. Here, we aim to review the human cerebrovascular phenotypes associated with ephrinB2-EphB4-RASA1 mutations, including those recently discovered in Vein of Galen malformation: the most common and severe brain arteriovenous malformation in neonates. We will also discuss emerging paradigms of the molecular and cellular pathophysiology of disease-causing ephrinB2-EphB4-RASA1 mutations, including the potential role of somatic mosaicism. These observations have potential diagnostic and therapeutic implications for patients with rare congenital cerebrovascular diseases and their families.

#### [A] Targeting PI3K/AKT/mTOR Pathway Exerts Inhibitory Effects on Endothelial Cells from Generalized Lymphatic Anomaly.

- PMID: 42929833
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 其他血管畸形/血管异常
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Cells
- 作者: Lorusso B, Finato L, Nogara A, Roti G, Montanaro A, Monica G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 13
- DOI: 10.3390/cells15201860
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42929833/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Generalized lymphatic anomaly (GLA) is a congenital, non-neoplastic vascular anomaly characterized by diffuse or multifocal involvement and infiltration of multiple organs. Most GLA cases harbor "hotspot" somatic missense single nucleotide mutations in the PIK3CA gene, whereas a minority involve mutations in other genes. Recently, rapamycin and its homologs have shown encouraging results in the treatment of alterations affecting the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway. Therefore, the present work aimed to expand the immunophenotypic characterization of the lymphatic lesion at the tissue level and to evaluate the in vitro viability, migration, and angiogenic capacity of endothelial cells freshly isolated from a PIK3CA-mutated GLA sample, following treatment with alpelisib, rapamycin, dactolisib, and buparlisib. We detected the p.Gln546Lys amino acid substitution in the PI3Kca helical domain in GLA-derived endothelial cells. Then, by immunohistochemical analysis, we documented altered expression of mTOR and its well-characterized downstream substrates, which regulate growth, proliferation, migration, angiogenesis, and survival of ECs. Finally, pharmacological treatment reduced GLA-EC functional properties and down-modulated the hyperactivated PI3K downstream substrates. Overall, our results suggest that routine immunohistochemistry, integrated with phospho-protein analysis and next-generation sequencing, might improve histopathological classification of vascular anomalies and facilitate the development of personalized targeted therapies, particularly in recurrent or surgically challenging cases.

#### [A] Epidemiology, diagnostics and treatment of vascular tumours and malformations.

- PMID: 24979522
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Advances in clinical and experimental medicine : official organ Wroclaw Medical University
- 作者: Wójcicki P, Wójcicka K
- 发表日期: 2014
- DOI: 10.17219/acem/37149
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24979522/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

Vascular tumours and vascular malformations are common vasculose anomalies characteristic for dissimilar clinical course, specific biological as well as immune cytological and histological properties. Vascular lesions classification system and their detailed division into groups and subgroups were elaborated and implemented in Rome, in 1996, during meeting of the International Society for the Study of Vascular Anomalies (ISSVA). It was based on modification of an earlier going division by Mullikien and Głowacki from 1982. Infantile hemangiomas are the most numerous group of benign tumours of mesenchymal origin. Vascular malformations appear definitely less often. They are composed of normal endothelium lined displastic vessels which originate from vascular tissue abnormal morphogenesis. In contrast, in hemangiomas, at the proliferation stage, increased, multiplication of endothelial cells is observed as well as of fibroblasts, mastocytes and macrophages. Infantile hemangiomas are usually not present at the moment of birth and white chloasma with superficial teleangiectasis appears which increases within 3-4 weeks and gets bright red colour and reveal very characteristic clinical course basing on intensive growth period and involution long process. Vascular malformations are observed most often at the delivery moment or they may appear at an early childhood. They enlarge proportionally along with the child's growth and their sudden expansion may be triggered by an infection, hormonal changes or trauma. Contrary to hemangiomas, they do not subside spontaneously and their abrupt increase may result in impairment or deformation of important anatomical structures. Infantile hemangiomas and vascular malformations require different and individual treatments which are often multi-stage procedures carried on in specialistic centres of plastic surgery, vascular surgery or maxillofacial surgery.

#### [B] [Klippel-Trenaunay syndrome and pregnancy: difficult choice of delivery from a case and a review of the literature].

- PMID: 24461340

- 作者: Koch A, Aïssi G, Gaudineau A, Sananes N, Murtada R, Favre R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2014 Sep
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jgyn.2013.11.016
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24461340/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Klippel-Trenaunay syndrome (SKT) is a rare disease characterized by cutaneous haemangiomas, capillary malformations venous (venous varicosities), bone and soft tissue hypertrophy affecting one or more limbs. It is often associated with hemorrhagic and thrombotic complications, especially during pregnancy. Vulvovaginal anomalies at risk of bleeding may occur in late trimester of pregnancy, affecting delivery. The terms of delivery depends on the severity of vascular malformations and the experience of the obstetrician. We report the case of a woman in labor at 38 weeks gestation with a SKT with involvement of the left leg up to the corresponding large lip. She presented after vaginal delivery a severe post-partum hemorrhage (2000mL) secondary to vaginal lacerations requiring sutures and supplemented by arterial embolization. A multidisciplinary approach is required at delivery.

#### 摘要

Vascular malformations affect the viscera less commonly than the head and neck, extremities, and extra-cavitary soft tissues. They present with a wide spectrum of symptoms and findings including pain, respiratory compromise, hemoptysis, chylothorax, ascites, gastrointestinal bleeding, and obstruction. Management options depend upon the subtype of malformation and anatomic extent and may include sclerotherapy, embolization, surgical extirpation, coloanal pull-through, and occasionally more innovative individualized surgical approaches.

#### [B] Contrast‑enhanced ultrasound-guided sclerotherapy of vascular malformations in the head and neck: Initial clinical experience in a paediatric case series.

- PMID: 42936113
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal

- 主题: 静脉畸形基础与治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Ultrasound (Leeds, England)
- 作者: Lentge F, Jehn P, Abu-Fares O, Linderkamp ML, Papazacharias E, Gellrich NC, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 25
- DOI: 10.1177/1742271X261495660
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42936113/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

PURPOSE: Vascular malformations of the head and neck pose therapeutic challenges due to complex anatomy and proximity to critical structures. Percutaneous sclerotherapy guided by digital subtraction angiography is effective for low-flow malformations but involves radiation and iodinated contrast. Contrast-enhanced ultrasound offers a radiation-free alternative with real-time visualisation of intralesional contrast.
METHODS: We retrospectively analysed contrast-enhanced ultrasound-guided sclerotherapy procedures for head and neck malformations in children under 6 years performed since 2021, assessing feasibility, safety and clinical outcomes. Six interventions in six patients (44 days to 3.5 years) were included.
RESULTS: Contrast-enhanced ultrasound confirmed intralesional needle placement in all cases without evidence of extralesional contrast leakage. Sclerotherapy with Fibrovein or Aethoxysklerol was successfully performed in every procedure. No contrast-related adverse events occurred and all patients showed at least temporary clinical improvement.
CONCLUSIONS: Contrast-enhanced ultrasound therefore appears feasible and safe for guiding sclerotherapy of venolymphatic malformations in the head and neck, enabling real-time verification without ionising radiation or iodinated contrast. The technique may be particularly valuable in paediatric patients requiring repeated interventions.

#### [B] Case Report: transient migratory pulmonary nodules with bronchial artery malformations in an autosomal dominant polycystic kidney disease patient: a case of recurrent hemoptysis.

- PMID: 42953645

- 作者: Hu Y, Zhang J, Wang T, Xue B
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fmed.2026.1949035
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42953645/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Autosomal dominant polycystic kidney disease (ADPKD) is associated with vascular malformations, but bronchial artery involvement with migratory pulmonary nodules has not been reported. We describe an 81-year-old man with recurrent hemoptysis and migratory nodules on chest CT. Digital subtraction angiography revealed tortuous, dilated bronchial arteries; embolization controlled bleeding and nodules resolved. He had polycystic kidney and liver disease, a prior intracranial aneurysm, and a PKD1 missense variant (c.8426C > T). Common causes of hemoptysis were excluded. This case describes co-occurrence of bronchial artery malformation, recurrent hemoptysis and migratory pulmonary nodules in a patient with genetically confirmed ADPKD, raising the possibility that bronchial arterial involvement could be a rare, under-recognized vascular manifestation associated with ADPKD.

#### [B] Intravascular papillary endothelial hyperplasia of larynx: case report and literature review of all head and neck cases.

- PMID: 24981303
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- DOI: 10.1016/j.otpol.2014.03.002
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24981303/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Intravascular papillary endothelial hyperplasia (IPEH) is a benign lesion of a vascular origin that is caused by excessive proliferation of endothelial cells in blood vessels or vascular malformations. It is a rare entity that can present in any region of the body, but with particular predilection to the head and neck region and the extremities. We also present the results of the English literature search, which to our knowledge are all the published cases of IPEH in the head and neck region (No=213). IPEH has not been reported to arise from the glottic region previously. We present a first case of IPEH arising from the vocal fold of a 48-year-old male. Histological differential diagnosis of IPEH includes several entities, most importantly angiosarcoma. Presentation and histology are discussed. The main treatment option is a complete surgical resection. Prognosis of IPEH is excellent, with the exception of some intracranial cases.

#### [B] Vascular anomalies of the hand and wrist.

- PMID: 24860131
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- 作者: Ek ET, Suh N, Carlson MG
- 发表日期: 2014 Jun
- DOI: 10.5435/JAAOS-22-06-352
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24860131/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Vascular malformations of the hand and wrist are uncommon. They develop from aberrations in angiogenic signaling during vascular development. Unlike hemangiomas, which are characterized by biphasic growth and slow spontaneous involution, vascular malformations continue to grow proportionally with the child. Management is dictated by classification of the vascular malformations, which is based on flow characteristics (ie, low, high) and predominant cell type (ie, venous, lymphatic, capillary, combined, arteriovenous). Initial management is conservative, with the goal of providing relief from pain and swelling. Sclerotherapy, laser treatment, and arterial embolization may be beneficial in well-selected patients. Surgery is indicated in cases of persistent pain and uncontrolled limb swelling leading to functional impairment and/or neurologic compression. The goals of surgery are to excise as much of the lesion as possible while avoiding injury to adjacent nerves, minimizing blood loss, and preventing distal limb ischemia. This mandates careful preoperative planning and meticulous technique. Adjuvant treatments may be warranted, as in the case of preoperative embolization in patients with high-flow lesions.

#### [B] Adult giant cerebellar cavernous malformations: case report and review of the literature.

- PMID: 34406083
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content

- 作者: Pongeluppi RI, de Souza SNF, Neder L, Furlanetti L, Colli BO, Ballestero MFM, et al.
- 发表日期: 2023 Dec
- DOI: 10.1080/02688697.2021.1961685
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34406083/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Cavernous malformations are vascular malformations that can occur anywhere in the central nervous system (CNS). Giant cavernous malformations (GCM) are extremely rare in adults, especially in the posterior fossa. Herein, we described a 48-year-old male who presented with vertigo and postural instability for three months. Neuroimaging revealed a 131.15 cm3 heterogeneous midline upper cerebellar lesion. After a suboccipital craniotomy, a gross total resection (GTR) was accomplished. Histopathologic examination revealed a huge cavernous malformation. Only 27 GCM adult cases were reported in the English-based literature. Only two patients had cerebellar lesions and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first case of cerebellar vermis GCM. We concluded that cerebellar GCM (CGCM) in adults are exceedingly rare and indolent lesions. These lesions can radiologically and clinically mimic neoplastic lesions that have to be considered in the differential diagnosis. GTR is the mainstay of treatment and, whenever possible, should be attempted.

- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1148/rg.250210
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42955840/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

Vascular abnormalities are less common in the hand and wrist than in the lower extremities but can result in significant morbidity if unrecognized or untreated. The unique vascular architecture of the hand and wrist, with a dense network of arterial anastomoses, confers resilience to ischemia, but the superficial location makes them vulnerable to trauma and occupational injury. The authors describe and illustrate a comprehensive multimodality imaging approach to the diagnosis and evaluation of vascular abnormalities of the hand and wrist. They begin with an overview of vascular anatomy and normal variants, including a persistent median artery. Multimodality imaging, including US, CT angiography, MR angiography, and digital subtraction angiography, is reviewed with a focus on strengths, limitations, and clinical utility. Dynamic imaging techniques and photon-counting CT are highlighted for their evolving roles. A wide spectrum of vascular diseases, including atherosclerosis, trauma, iatrogenic injury, embolism, hypothenar hammer syndrome, hand-arm vibration syndrome, vasculitis, and frostbite, as well as their imaging appearances, are discussed. Vascular anomalies, including vascular malformations and vascular neoplasms, are also reviewed. The role of imaging in hand transplantation is addressed. The authors provide a framework for recognizing vascular diseases in the hand and wrist and selecting the appropriate imaging modality. Familiarity with vascular anatomy, imaging findings, and clinical context is essential for timely diagnosis, guiding management, and preserving limb function.

#### [B] Advanced CT Myelography for Spontaneous Cerebrospinal Fluid Leaks.

#### 摘要

Spontaneous intracranial hypotension is a frequently debilitating condition that is most commonly caused by loss of cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) from the spine. The most frequently occurring types of spontaneous spinal CSF leaks that cause spontaneous intracranial hypotension are ventral dural tears, lateral dural tears, and CSF venous fistulas, but other leak types are recognized including sacral dural tears or fistulas into vascular malformations. Localization and characterization of spinal CSF leaks with CT myelography facilitates subsequent targeted treatment. A tailored approach based on the suspected leak type as determined at MRI of the spine is necessary to maximize the likelihood of a diagnostic study. The authors focus primarily on advanced CT myelographic techniques for leaks from dural tears and CSF venous fistulas. The importance of optimal patient positioning, typical and atypical appearances of the different leak types, and artifacts that can mimic a CSF leak are considered. ©RSNA, 2026 Supplemental material is available for this article. See the invited commentary by Madhavan in this issue.

#### [B] Pulmonary Developmental Disorders in Adults: An Integrative Review of Embryology, Pathogenesis, Imaging, and Management.

- PMID: 42438382
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation without a strong targeted mechanism signal
- 优先级依据: B: CCM/related vascular-malformation clinical evidence
- 主题: 静脉畸形经典文献/综述补位
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of thoracic imaging
- 作者: Ghosh S, Sykes AG, Ferreira A, Kruger LF, Hahn LD, Bratt AK, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov 01
- DOI: 10.1097/RTI.0000000000000895
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42438382/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

Pulmonary developmental disorders comprise a heterogeneous group of congenital abnormalities involving the airways, lung parenchyma, pulmonary vasculature, or overlapping developmental pathways. Although these conditions commonly present during infancy or childhood, many remain clinically silent until adulthood, when they are discovered incidentally or manifest with recurrent respiratory symptoms such as infection, hemoptysis, or progressive dyspnea. These abnormalities may be broadly categorized into airway, vascular, parenchymal, and mixed developmental anomalies. Airway abnormalities include congenital tracheobronchomegaly, branching anomalies of the tracheobronchial tree, bronchial atresia, bronchogenic cysts, congenital pulmonary airway malformations (CPAM), and bronchopulmonary sequestration (BPS). Vascular anomalies include pulmonary artery sling, proximal interruption of the pulmonary artery, Scimitar syndrome, and meandering pulmonary veins. Parenchymal disorders encompass congenital lobar overinflation and pulmonary hypoplasia. Increasing recognition of overlapping lesions, particularly combinations of CPAM, sequestration, and bronchial atresia, has challenged traditional compartmental classification systems and supports a developmental spectrum model. This review synthesizes contemporary concepts regarding embryologic mechanisms, pathogenesis, imaging findings, and management of these entities in adults. Particular emphasis is placed on imaging evaluation with CT and MRI, updated terminology, evolving molecular understanding of CPAMs, including lesions with malignant potential, and distinctions between congenital sequestration and pseudosequestration. Recognition of these disorders and their associated anomalies is essential for accurate diagnosis, appropriate management, and surgical planning in adult patients.

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24889662/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Cyanoacrylates are a group of fast-acting adhesives. They form low viscosity liquids in the monomer state and instantly polymerize to become adhesive upon contact with ionic substances. Since the 1950s, they have been used around the world for industrial and household purposes. N-butyl cyanoacrylate (NBCA) is a cyanoacrylate that is commonly used for medical care, and the closure of skin wounds with NBCA has been found to promote hemostasis. However, in Japan, the intravascular injection of NBCA is considered to be off-label use, except during the treatment of gastric varices under endoscopy. The use of NBCA in embolotherapy is considered when the target vessels cannot be cannulated superselectively, for vascular diseases that require long segments of the target vessel to be embolized, or for patients in a hypocoagulable state. NBCA-based embolotherapy can be used to treat vascular malformations, acute hemorrhaging, tumors, and venous disease. The complications associated with NBCA-based embolotherapy include tissue ischemia, hemorrhaging, systemic or local reactions, and catheter adhesion to blood vessels. NBCA is mixed with Lipiodol to make it radiopaque and to adjust its polymerization time. Since there are various technical aspects to performing NBCA-based embolotherapy safely, it should be carried out by, or with the assistance of, proficient interventional radiologists.

#### [A] 3D-printed fish-skin-derived composite dECM hydrogel patch for immunomodulatory and pro-angiogenic diabetic wound repair.

- PMID: 42927160
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Materials today. Bio
- 作者: Lee SH, Moon JS, Noh S, Yi HS, Lee H
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.mtbio.2026.103753
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42927160/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-26

#### 摘要

Diabetic wounds are sustained by persistent inflammation, oxidative stress, infection susceptibility, and impaired angiogenesis, highlighting the need for bioactive dressings with manufacturable architectures. Here, we developed a methacrylation-free and photopolymerization-free fish-derived composite decellularized extracellular matrix (dECM) hydrogel scaffold by blending tilapia skin-derived dECM (TdECM) with carp skin-derived dECM (CdECM). Rheology-guided screening identified TdECM/CdECM-2, a 1:2 (v/v) blend of TdECM and CdECM, as a printable formulation that combined shear-thinning behavior, thermally induced collagen fibrillogenesis, and extrusion printability. Post-print stabilization with 1-ethyl-3-(3-dimethylaminopropyl)carbodiimide (EDC) and N-hydroxysuccinimide (NHS) preserved lattice architecture and generated highly porous scaffolds with a porosity of 90.3 ± 3.9%. In vitro, the composite scaffold (TdECM/CdECM-2) supported fibroblast, keratinocyte, and endothelial cell growth; promoted keratinocyte migration and endothelial network formation; and reduced Staphylococcus aureus and Pseudomonas aeruginosa survival to 10.5 ± 1.3% and 13.3 ± 3.3%, respectively, in direct-contact assays. Macrophage studies indicated attenuated pro-inflammatory gene expression, increased pro-regenerative macrophage-associated markers, and reduced oxidative stress-associated reactive oxygen species accumulation. In mice with streptozotocin-induced diabetes, TdECM/CdECM-2 increased wound closure to 91.2 ± 3.9% at day 14, compared with 73.2 ± 9.7% in the untreated control and 79.3 ± 12.5% in the porcine skin-derived dECM (PdECM) group, together with improved re-epithelialization, collagen deposition, and cluster of differentiation 31 (CD31)-positive neovascularization, and reduced inflammatory myeloid cell accumulation and macrophage activation. Exploratory single-cell transcriptomic profiling suggested treatment-associated modulation of immune and stromal/endothelial gene programs related to inflammation and angiogenesis. These findings support fish-derived composite dECM scaffolds as printable bioactive wound dressing platforms for diabetic wound repair.

#### [A] A biomimetic gas-protein co-delivery nanoplatform targets mitochondrial stress and GSDME activation for glioblastoma immunotherapy.

- PMID: 42926787
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Materials today. Bio
- 作者: Ma G, Li J, Wang K, Yu D, Li J, Guan M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.mtbio.2026.103750
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42926787/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-26

#### 摘要

Glioblastoma (GBM) remains one of the most aggressive primary brain malignancies, with the therapeutic efficacy of conventional treatments and cancer immunotherapy being severely hindered by the blood-brain barrier (BBB) and the highly immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME). Although pyroptosis, an inflammatory form of programmed cell death, can elicit potent antitumor immunity through the release of damage-associated molecular patterns (DAMPs), the precise induction of pyroptosis and the amplification of its immunostimulatory effects remain major challenges. Herein, we developed a biomimetic microglial cell membrane-coated nanosystem (M@GM) that integrates brain-targeted delivery, mitochondria-mediated damage, and innate immune activation for cascade-enhanced glioblastoma therapy. The nanosystem consists of a manganese carbonyl (MnCO) core loaded with Gasdermin E (GSDME) and cloaked with a microglial cell membrane, enabling efficient BBB penetration and active GBM targeting. Upon accumulation within the tumor microenvironment, M@GM responsively releases CO, Mn2+, and GSDME. CO and Mn2+ induced mitochondrial stress and ROS accumulation, which were accompanied by activation of the Caspase-3/GSDME axis and pyroptosis-associated cell death. The associated HMGB1 release and CRT exposure are consistent with an immunogenic cell death (ICD)-related response, while Mn2+ cooperatively activates the cGAS-STING innate immune pathway, promoting dendritic cell maturation, effector T-cell infiltration, and M1 polarization of tumor-associated macrophages, ultimately converting the immunologically "cold" GBM microenvironment into an immune-active "hot" phenotype. Both in vitro and in vivo studies demonstrated enhanced BBB transport and brain accumulation of M@GM, significantly inhibited GBM progression, prolonged the survival of tumor-bearing mice, and exhibited favorable biosafety. This work presents a biomimetic therapeutic strategy based on "biomimetic delivery-pyroptosis amplification-immune remodeling", providing a promising design paradigm for brain-targeted nanomedicine-enabled cancer immunotherapy.

#### [A] Laparoscopic and endoscopic-assisted resection of small bowel venous malformations in blue rubber bleb nevus syndrome (BRBNS): a technical case report with operative video.

- PMID: 42931919

- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42931919/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Blue Rubber Bleb Nevus Syndrome (BRBNS) is a rare condition characterized by venous malformations in multiple organ systems, most commonly presenting with cutaneous lesions in early childhood followed by complications from gastrointestinal bleeding in adolescence and early adulthood. Once symptomatic, this condition is often managed with endoscopic techniques and medical therapies that inhibit angiogenesis. However, patients may require surgical management when endoscopic approaches are not feasible, or if patients continue to have persistent gastrointestinal bleeding after maximizing endoscopic and medical therapy. Although surgery can achieve complete resection of gastrointestinal venous malformations, determining the optimal operative approach may be challenging. Lesions can vary substantially in size, depth, location, and extent of bowel involvement. As a result, some patients may require highly invasive procedures, including resections associated with substantial loss of bowel length. Given the early age at presentation and the potential need for future interventions, careful documentation of preoperative evaluation and minimally invasive approaches for managing gastrointestinal lesions associated with BRBNS is essential. In this technical case report, we describe a combined minimally invasive laparoscopic and intraoperative endoscopic approach for the treatment of small bowel venous malformations associated with BRBNS in an adolescent patient. Although feasible in this case, BRBNS remains a rare and heterogeneous condition that may still require invasive surgical management. Further studies are needed to better define the natural history of this condition and long-term outcomes, particularly in the absence of a curative treatment and the early age at which many patients are affected.

#### [A] Targeting PI3K/AKT/mTOR Pathway Exerts Inhibitory Effects on Endothelial Cells from Generalized Lymphatic Anomaly.

- DOI: 10.3390/cells15201860
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42929833/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Generalized lymphatic anomaly (GLA) is a congenital, non-neoplastic vascular anomaly characterized by diffuse or multifocal involvement and infiltration of multiple organs. Most GLA cases harbor "hotspot" somatic missense single nucleotide mutations in the PIK3CA gene, whereas a minority involve mutations in other genes. Recently, rapamycin and its homologs have shown encouraging results in the treatment of alterations affecting the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway. Therefore, the present work aimed to expand the immunophenotypic characterization of the lymphatic lesion at the tissue level and to evaluate the in vitro viability, migration, and angiogenic capacity of endothelial cells freshly isolated from a PIK3CA-mutated GLA sample, following treatment with alpelisib, rapamycin, dactolisib, and buparlisib. We detected the p.Gln546Lys amino acid substitution in the PI3Kca helical domain in GLA-derived endothelial cells. Then, by immunohistochemical analysis, we documented altered expression of mTOR and its well-characterized downstream substrates, which regulate growth, proliferation, migration, angiogenesis, and survival of ECs. Finally, pharmacological treatment reduced GLA-EC functional properties and down-modulated the hyperactivated PI3K downstream substrates. Overall, our results suggest that routine immunohistochemistry, integrated with phospho-protein analysis and next-generation sequencing, might improve histopathological classification of vascular anomalies and facilitate the development of personalized targeted therapies, particularly in recurrent or surgically challenging cases.

#### [A] EXPRESS: Brain on fire: Why intracerebral hemorrhage requires a firefighting strategy beyond single therapeutic targets.

- PMID: 42928100
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of cerebral blood flow and metabolism : official journal of the International Society of Cerebral Blood Flow and Metabolism
- 作者: Zille M
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 25
- DOI: 10.1177/0271678X261503504
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42928100/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH) remains the most lethal form of stroke and lacks an established neuroprotective therapy. Secondary injury after ICH reflects interacting processes, including hematoma expansion, hemolysis-derived toxicity, neuroinflammation, blood-brain barrier dysfunction, perihematomal edema, oxidative stress, and regulated cell death. Although mechanistic single-target studies remain essential for defining biological relevance, treatment windows, target engagement, and safety, ICH is unlikely to be adequately addressed by any one intervention alone. Using the analogy of a "fire in the brain," this perspective proposes a coordinated, time-sensitive firefighting framework: Rapid blood-pressure management and hemostatic reversal can limit continued bleeding. Hematoma evacuation can remove biological fuel in selected patients. Interventions targeting lipid peroxidation and oxidative injury may limit secondary combustion. Inflammation- and edema-directed therapies may contain harmful heat. Enhancement of endogenous hematoma clearance may promote resolution and repair. Imaging and biomarkers can support phenotyping, monitoring, and treatment selection. Emerging signals from care bundle, minimally invasive evacuation, and experimental combination studies support the testing of staged, biologically rational add-on strategies rather than fixed therapeutic cocktails. The goal is not to deploy all the tools at once, but to match appropriate interventions to the evolving biology, phenotype, timing, feasibility, and care context of each hemorrhage.

#### [A] Microbial serotonin signalling axes in irritable bowel syndrome: integrative molecular crosstalk among enterochromaffin cells, gut microbiota, neuroimmune circuits, and precision therapeutic engineering.

- PMID: 42928090
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Archives of microbiology
- 作者: Mehra RK, Singh BP, Siddiqui S, Aldawsari MF, Kamal MA, Anwer MK
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 25
- DOI: 10.1007/s00203-026-05250-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42928090/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Irritable bowel syndrome (IBS) is a common, often disabling disorder of gut-brain interaction whose molecular basis remains incompletely defined, leaving diagnosis largely symptom-based and treatment largely non-mechanistic. We argue that the serotonin (5-hydroxytryptamine; hereafter 5-HT, the primary term used throughout, with "serotonin" retained in less technical contexts) signalling axis-positioned at the interface of the gut microbiota, enterochromaffin (EC) cells, enteric neurons, and the mucosal immune system-is a central, unifying integrator of IBS pathophysiology, and that reframing IBS around this axis offers a route toward mechanism-based subtyping and therapy. We first synthesise how microbiota-driven tryptophan metabolism, short-chain fatty acid and secondary bile acid signalling, and epigenetic regulation of tryptophan hydroxylase 1 (TPH1) shape EC-cell serotonin output, before examining the neuroimmune consequences of dysregulated serotonin secretion-mast cell activation, macrophage polarisation, neurogenic inflammation-and the microbiota-serotonin-gut-brain connectome linking vagal afferent signalling, blood-brain barrier permeability, and central neurotransmitter networks. We then discuss molecular stratification of IBS subtypes through serotonergic endotyping-incorporating SLC6A4 and TPH1 polymorphisms, single-cell transcriptomics, and spatial metabolomics-and critically evaluate next-generation therapeutic strategies, including engineered probiotic chassis, CRISPR-mediated microbial editing, and AI-guided nanomedicine, distinguishing proof-of-concept findings from clinically validated approaches. We conclude that reconceptualising IBS as a disorder of disrupted microbial serotonergic ecosystems, rather than a purely symptom-defined syndrome, offers a coherent and testable framework for biomarker discovery and precision, mechanism-directed therapy, while highlighting the causal-directionality and clinical-translation gaps that must be closed before this framework can be applied at the bedside.

#### [A] From latency to neurodegeneration: varicella-zoster virus reactivation and dementia risk, with a focus on herpes zoster ophthalmicus.

- PMID: 42955572
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Journal of neurovirology
- 作者: Samalia PD, Wang R, Brickell K, Netzler N, Meyer J, Niederer RL
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 29
- DOI: 10.1007/s13365-026-01351-5
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42955572/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Varicella zoster virus (VZV) reactivation has emerged as a potential contributor to dementia through vascular and inflammatory mechanisms, with herpes zoster ophthalmicus (HZO) representing a potentially higher-risk phenotype because of its association with cerebrovascular complications. This narrative review evaluated the epidemiological and mechanistic evidence linking VZV reactivation to cognitive decline. MEDLINE, PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Library were searched from inception to March 2026, supplemented by relevant publications identified during revision through October 2026, with emphasis on systematic reviews, large observational studies, quasi-experimental vaccination studies, and mechanistic investigations. Infection-based studies demonstrated heterogeneous findings, with modest associations between herpes zoster and subsequent dementia that frequently attenuated after adjustment for frailty and comorbidity. In contrast, vaccination studies, particularly natural experiments and quasi-experimental analyses, consistently reported lower dementia incidence following zoster immunisation, although causal interpretation and the magnitude of any protective effect remain uncertain. Observational studies suggest that antiviral therapy is associated with fewer short-term cerebrovascular complications, although its relationship with long-term cognitive outcomes remains uncertain. VZV-associated vasculopathy provides the strongest mechanistic rationale for a link with cognitive decline, whereas neuroinflammation, possible blood-brain barrier dysfunction, and interactions with neurodegenerative processes remain proposed pathways. Current evidence suggests that VZV reactivation may accelerate cognitive decline in susceptible individuals, but causality remains unproven. Further prospective studies are needed to clarify the contribution of VZV reactivation and the potential neuroprotective effects of vaccination and antiviral therapy.

#### [A] Clostridium perfringens Delta toxin targets CD9 to drive NEC and brain injury.

- PMID: 42951958
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate

- 来源说明: Cell/Nature/Science生命科学顶刊
- 期刊: Nature
- 作者: Zhang S, Gong X, Tian S, Yang Y, Tong X, Sheng K, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.1038/s41586-026-11211-1
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42951958/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) is the most devastating gastrointestinal emergency in preterm infants, frequently causing mortality and long-term neurodevelopmental impairment1,2. Although the gut microbiota has been implicated in NEC pathogenesis3-5, the microbial factors driving NEC and the following brain pathology remain unclear. Here we identify Clostridium perfringens pathobionts containing Delta toxin in the gut microbiome of individuals with severe and fatal NEC. Clinically, infected infants show worse outcomes, including aberrant systemic inflammation, severe neurological damage and coagulopathy. We establish a mouse model in which Delta-toxin-positive C. perfringens infection, or Delta toxin exposure alone, recapitulates clinical features of NEC, including intestinal necrosis and acute cerebral injury. Mechanistically, Delta toxin preferentially targets macrophages, potently activating inflammasome signalling and pyroptosis, triggering a rapid inflammatory cascade that compromises blood-brain barrier integrity and drives neuroinflammation. Through a genome-wide CRISPR screen, we identify the tetraspanin CD9 as the host receptor for Delta toxin. Using systemic, myeloid-specific and intestinal-epithelial-specific Cd9-knockout mice, we show that systemic and myeloid Cd9 ablation protects mice against toxin-induced lethality, blood-brain barrier dysfunction and multiorgan injury, whereas epithelial Cd9 deletion shows partial protection, implicating macrophage targeting as a primary driver of C. perfringens CD9-dependent pathogenesis. Furthermore, a neutralizing anti-Delta toxin monoclonal antibody confers protection against disease progression. These findings suggest that a subset of NEC is a specific-toxin-mediated disorder and validate the Delta toxin-CD9 axis as a therapeutic target for this subset.

#### [A] Architectural Regulation of T-Lymphocyte Function in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: Current Evidence and Translational Opportunities.

- PMID: 42910499

- 作者: Abdullah MH, Zulkifle N, Suhaimi SA, Aziz MY, Mohamed R
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 31
- DOI: 10.1096/fj.202603114R
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42910499/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) develops within a chronically injured liver in which anti-tumor immunity is governed not only by immune-cell abundance and checkpoint signaling, but also by the spatial, mechanical, and multicellular organization of T lymphocytes. This review reframes T-cell architecture as a regulatory layer operating across molecular, cellular, and tissue scales. Cytoskeletal remodeling, immunological synapse geometry, mechanotransduction, vascular positioning, stromal exclusion, and tertiary lymphoid structure (TLS) organization collectively influence T-cell activation thresholds, effector competence, exhaustion trajectories, and therapeutic responsiveness. We synthesize HCC-specific evidence from spatial transcriptomics, multiplex imaging, digital pathology, and clinical cohorts, while clearly distinguishing direct HCC observations from mechanisms extrapolated from other solid tumors or foundational T-cell biology. Current evidence supports the prognostic relevance of intratumoral CD8+ localisation, perivascular immune rims, fibrotic exclusion niches, and mature intratumoral TLS, but causal links between architecture and T-cell fate remain incompletely defined. We propose an architecture-guided framework in which spatial immune states serve as both biomarkers and modifiable therapeutic targets. Spatially informed checkpoint blockade, engineered T-cell therapy, stromal and vascular remodeling, AI-assisted pathology, and organoid or liver-on-chip models may enable more precise immunotherapy strategies for HCC.

#### [A] Integrated Network Pharmacology and Experimental Validation Implicate AKT1-Associated Signaling in the Protective Effects of Panax notoginseng Against Pulmonary Arterial Hypertension.

- PMID: 42959756

- DOI: 10.1002/pul2.70435
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959756/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH) is a progressive and fatal cardiopulmonary disease characterized by pulmonary vascular remodeling, endothelial dysfunction, inflammation, and increased pulmonary vascular resistance. Despite advances in targeted therapies, effective treatment options remain limited. This study investigated the molecular mechanisms underlying the therapeutic effects of Panax notoginseng against PAH through integrated computational and experimental validation. Network pharmacology was employed to identify the bioactive compounds and therapeutic targets of Panax notoginseng. Functional enrichment, protein-protein interaction network analysis, transcriptomic validation, molecular docking, surface plasmon resonance (SPR), molecular dynamics simulation, and MM/PBSA analyses were performed to characterize representative target-compound interactions. Experimental validation to predict the therapeutic mechanism was performed using RAW264.7 macrophages, pulmonary artery smooth muscle cells (PASMCs), and a Sugen5416/hypoxia-induced murine model of PAH. A total of 67 overlapping therapeutic targets were identified, with enrichment analyses highlighting inflammation, oxidative stress, apoptosis, vascular remodeling, and the PI3K/AKT signaling pathway. AKT1 was identified as a central hub gene and was significantly upregulated under hypoxic conditions. Quercetin and Liquiritigenin (DFV)exhibited stable interactions with AKT1, with quercetin demonstrating stronger binding affinity (-11.10 kcal/mol) than liquiritigenin (-9.33 kcal/mol). Consistent with these computational predictions, Panax notoginseng reduced AKT1 expression and phosphorylation, attenuated oxidative stress and inflammatory cytokine production, inhibited PASMC proliferation and migration, and significantly improved pulmonary hemodynamics, right ventricular hypertrophy, and pulmonary vascular remodeling in vivo. Collectively, these findings identify AKT1 as a key molecular target through which Panax notoginseng exerts therapeutic effects against PAH in vivo.

#### [A] Panax notoginseng-Derived Carbon Quantum Dots as a Novel Administration Strategy for Cerebral Ischemia-Reperfusion Injury Therapy.
