### glycolysis / mitochondria / metabolic reprogramming（1）

#### [A] A biomimetic gas-protein co-delivery nanoplatform targets mitochondrial stress and GSDME activation for glioblastoma immunotherapy.

- PMID: 42926787
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Materials today. Bio
- 作者: Ma G, Li J, Wang K, Yu D, Li J, Guan M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec
- DOI: 10.1016/j.mtbio.2026.103750
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42926787/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-26

#### 摘要

Glioblastoma (GBM) remains one of the most aggressive primary brain malignancies, with the therapeutic efficacy of conventional treatments and cancer immunotherapy being severely hindered by the blood-brain barrier (BBB) and the highly immunosuppressive tumor microenvironment (TME). Although pyroptosis, an inflammatory form of programmed cell death, can elicit potent antitumor immunity through the release of damage-associated molecular patterns (DAMPs), the precise induction of pyroptosis and the amplification of its immunostimulatory effects remain major challenges. Herein, we developed a biomimetic microglial cell membrane-coated nanosystem (M@GM) that integrates brain-targeted delivery, mitochondria-mediated damage, and innate immune activation for cascade-enhanced glioblastoma therapy. The nanosystem consists of a manganese carbonyl (MnCO) core loaded with Gasdermin E (GSDME) and cloaked with a microglial cell membrane, enabling efficient BBB penetration and active GBM targeting. Upon accumulation within the tumor microenvironment, M@GM responsively releases CO, Mn2+, and GSDME. CO and Mn2+ induced mitochondrial stress and ROS accumulation, which were accompanied by activation of the Caspase-3/GSDME axis and pyroptosis-associated cell death. The associated HMGB1 release and CRT exposure are consistent with an immunogenic cell death (ICD)-related response, while Mn2+ cooperatively activates the cGAS-STING innate immune pathway, promoting dendritic cell maturation, effector T-cell infiltration, and M1 polarization of tumor-associated macrophages, ultimately converting the immunologically "cold" GBM microenvironment into an immune-active "hot" phenotype. Both in vitro and in vivo studies demonstrated enhanced BBB transport and brain accumulation of M@GM, significantly inhibited GBM progression, prolonged the survival of tumor-bearing mice, and exhibited favorable biosafety. This work presents a biomimetic therapeutic strategy based on "biomimetic delivery-pyroptosis amplification-immune remodeling", providing a promising design paradigm for brain-targeted nanomedicine-enabled cancer immunotherapy.

### PI3K-AKT-mTOR / targeted therapy（5）

#### [A] Targeting PI3K/AKT/mTOR Pathway Exerts Inhibitory Effects on Endothelial Cells from Generalized Lymphatic Anomaly.

- PMID: 42929833
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 其他血管畸形/血管异常
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Cells
- 作者: Lorusso B, Finato L, Nogara A, Roti G, Montanaro A, Monica G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 13
- DOI: 10.3390/cells15201860
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42929833/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Generalized lymphatic anomaly (GLA) is a congenital, non-neoplastic vascular anomaly characterized by diffuse or multifocal involvement and infiltration of multiple organs. Most GLA cases harbor "hotspot" somatic missense single nucleotide mutations in the PIK3CA gene, whereas a minority involve mutations in other genes. Recently, rapamycin and its homologs have shown encouraging results in the treatment of alterations affecting the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway. Therefore, the present work aimed to expand the immunophenotypic characterization of the lymphatic lesion at the tissue level and to evaluate the in vitro viability, migration, and angiogenic capacity of endothelial cells freshly isolated from a PIK3CA-mutated GLA sample, following treatment with alpelisib, rapamycin, dactolisib, and buparlisib. We detected the p.Gln546Lys amino acid substitution in the PI3Kca helical domain in GLA-derived endothelial cells. Then, by immunohistochemical analysis, we documented altered expression of mTOR and its well-characterized downstream substrates, which regulate growth, proliferation, migration, angiogenesis, and survival of ECs. Finally, pharmacological treatment reduced GLA-EC functional properties and down-modulated the hyperactivated PI3K downstream substrates. Overall, our results suggest that routine immunohistochemistry, integrated with phospho-protein analysis and next-generation sequencing, might improve histopathological classification of vascular anomalies and facilitate the development of personalized targeted therapies, particularly in recurrent or surgically challenging cases.

#### [A] Arteriovenous Malformations as Neoplastic-Like Lesions: Molecular Underpinnings and Implications for the Interventional Radiologist.

- PMID: 42934295
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形经典文献/综述补位
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Cardiovascular and interventional radiology
- 作者: Shashi KK, Gonzalez Garcia A, Sabado J, Richter GT, Tran AD, Chewning R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 26
- DOI: 10.1007/s00270-026-04650-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42934295/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-29

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) are high-flow vascular anomalies traditionally treated as congenital structural lesions with embolization or surgical resection. However, recurrence after embolization alone exceeds 90%, underscoring incomplete understanding of their biology. Next-generation sequencing has identified somatic gain-of-function mutations in KRAS, MAP2K1, BRAF, and HRAS as drivers of sporadic AVMs. Confined to lesional endothelial cells, these mutations activate RAS/MAPK signaling and support a neoplastic-like rather than purely structural disease model. This has therapeutic implications: MEK inhibitors, particularly trametinib, have shown angiographic and clinical responses in refractory AVMs. Interventional radiologists can obtain draining vein liquid-biopsy samples during embolization for molecular genotyping; embolization may increase circulating cell-free DNA yield. This review summarizes the molecular rationale, targeted therapy evidence, and integration of genotyping into embolization workflows to enable combined treatment strategies.Level of Evidence: No level of evidence assigned (Review Article).

#### [A] Inflammation and thrombosis: from pathophysiology to future treatments.

- PMID: 42955694
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Internal and emergency medicine
- 作者: Santagata D, Giovenali L, Guerrieri E, Falsetti L, Squizzato A, Moroncini G
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.1007/s11739-026-04574-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42955694/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Inflammation and coagulation are tightly interconnected host-defense systems that, when dysregulated, promote pathological thrombosis. This narrative review examines mechanisms, clinical settings, and therapeutic implications of thromboinflammation (TI) across venous, arterial, and microvascular thrombosis, with particular attention to the differences in clinical translation among vascular territories. Endothelial activation, tissue factor expression, platelet-leukocyte interactions, neutrophil extracellular traps, complement activation, inflammasome signalling, and impaired fibrinolysis converge to generate a procoagulant phenotype. These mechanisms modify Virchow's triad and contribute to venous, arterial, and microvascular thrombosis, although their relative importance varies across vascular territories. Clinically, TI is relevant in inflammatory bowel disease, rheumatologic and other immune-mediated disorders, sepsis, coronavirus disease 2019, clonal haematopoiesis, complement-mediated diseases, and atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease. TI offers a unifying framework for mechanistic risk assessment and therapeutic development. In atherothrombosis, randomised trials have demonstrated that selective modulation of inflammatory pathways, particularly the NLRP3-interleukin-1β-interleukin-6 axis and colchicine-sensitive pathways, can reduce recurrent cardiovascular events. By contrast, the role of inflammation-targeted therapies for primary or secondary prevention of VTE remains uncertain. Future studies should identify disease-specific biomarkers and test targeted anti-inflammatory or immunothrombotic strategies, alone or alongside anticoagulation, to prevent thrombosis without compromising haemostasis effectively.

Quercetin and Liquiritigenin (DFV)exhibited stable interactions with AKT1, with quercetin demonstrating stronger binding affinity (-11.10 kcal/mol) than liquiritigenin (-9.33 kcal/mol). Consistent with these computational predictions, Panax notoginseng reduced AKT1 expression and phosphorylation, attenuated oxidative stress and inflammatory cytokine production, inhibited PASMC proliferation and migration, and significantly improved pulmonary hemodynamics, right ventricular hypertrophy, and pulmonary vascular remodeling in vivo. Collectively, these findings identify AKT1 as a key molecular target through which Panax notoginseng exerts therapeutic effects against PAH in vivo.

#### [A] CCA, a cassane diterpene from Caesalpinia bonduc ameliorates cognition impairment in Alzheimer's disease mice by regulating neuroinflammation and autophagy.

- PMID: 42951505
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Chinese journal of natural medicines
- 作者: Zhou J, Zhang W, Yang Y, Jin Y, Li X, Wang Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Nov
- DOI: 10.1016/S1875-5364(26)61228-9
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42951505/
- 日报日期: 2026-11-01

#### 摘要

Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a severe neurodegenerative disorder, with persistent neuroinflammation and impaired autophagy recognized as key contributing factors. CCA is a naturally occurring cassane diterpene compound derived from the seed of Caesalpinia bonduc. Our research is the first to explore the role and mechanism of CCA in treating Alzheimer's disease. We assessed the levels of pro-inflammatory cytokines and autophagy in BV2 cells following lipopolysaccharide (LPS) treatment and anti-neuroinflammatory effects of CCA were evaluated in LPS-induced mice, with the underlying mechanisms explored using transcriptomic. Additionally, we examined the cognitive-enhancing effects of CCA in APP/PS1 mice and investigated Caesalpinia bonduc associated molecular mechanisms through proteomic analysis, immunohistochemistry (IHC) and immunofluorescence (IF) assays. Finally, the molecular target of CCA was validated using molecular dynamics simulations and cellular thermal shift assay (CETSA). CCA can down-regulate the mRNA levels of inflammatory factors in BV2 cells stimulated by LPS, and has good permeability of blood-brain barrier. Furthermore, CCA ameliorated cognitive impairment and neuronal damage in both LPS-induced and APP/PS1 mice. And demonstrated that CCA mitigates neuroinflammation by driving microglial polarization from M1 to M2, a process potentially mediated by the AMPK/NF-κB pathway, thereby reducing tau pathology. Proteomic profiling and IHC revealed that CCA upregulates FOXG1 expression in APP/PS1 mice. Furthermore, the scTenifoldKnk confirmed that AMPK has a significant impact on the regulation of autophagy and inflammation response. Our findings verified that CCA exhibits anti-neuroinflammatory activity by AMPK/NF-κB pathway and activates autophagy via the AMPK-mTOR-FOXG1 signaling axis, which underscores its potential as a novel therapeutic option for the treatment of AD.

### mTOR feedback / inhibitor resistance（4）

#### [A] Targeting PI3K/AKT/mTOR Pathway Exerts Inhibitory Effects on Endothelial Cells from Generalized Lymphatic Anomaly.

- PMID: 42929833
- Domain: Other vascular malformation
- Domain 依据: Other vascular malformation/anomaly expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 其他血管畸形/血管异常
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 期刊: Cells
- 作者: Lorusso B, Finato L, Nogara A, Roti G, Montanaro A, Monica G, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 13
- DOI: 10.3390/cells15201860
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42929833/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Generalized lymphatic anomaly (GLA) is a congenital, non-neoplastic vascular anomaly characterized by diffuse or multifocal involvement and infiltration of multiple organs. Most GLA cases harbor "hotspot" somatic missense single nucleotide mutations in the PIK3CA gene, whereas a minority involve mutations in other genes. Recently, rapamycin and its homologs have shown encouraging results in the treatment of alterations affecting the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway. Therefore, the present work aimed to expand the immunophenotypic characterization of the lymphatic lesion at the tissue level and to evaluate the in vitro viability, migration, and angiogenic capacity of endothelial cells freshly isolated from a PIK3CA-mutated GLA sample, following treatment with alpelisib, rapamycin, dactolisib, and buparlisib. We detected the p.Gln546Lys amino acid substitution in the PI3Kca helical domain in GLA-derived endothelial cells. Then, by immunohistochemical analysis, we documented altered expression of mTOR and its well-characterized downstream substrates, which regulate growth, proliferation, migration, angiogenesis, and survival of ECs. Finally, pharmacological treatment reduced GLA-EC functional properties and down-modulated the hyperactivated PI3K downstream substrates. Overall, our results suggest that routine immunohistochemistry, integrated with phospho-protein analysis and next-generation sequencing, might improve histopathological classification of vascular anomalies and facilitate the development of personalized targeted therapies, particularly in recurrent or surgically challenging cases.

#### [A] Arteriovenous Malformations as Neoplastic-Like Lesions: Molecular Underpinnings and Implications for the Interventional Radiologist.

- PMID: 42934295
- Domain: Brain AVM / AVM
- Domain 依据: Arteriovenous malformation expression in article content
- 转化相关性: Strong
- 转化相关性依据: Related vascular malformation with a transferable mechanism or targeted therapy
- 优先级依据: A: related vascular malformation + transferable mechanism/targeted therapy
- 主题: 静脉畸形经典文献/综述补位
- 来源类型: 经典补位
- 期刊: Cardiovascular and interventional radiology
- 作者: Shashi KK, Gonzalez Garcia A, Sabado J, Richter GT, Tran AD, Chewning R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 26
- DOI: 10.1007/s00270-026-04650-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42934295/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-29

#### 摘要

Arteriovenous malformations (AVMs) are high-flow vascular anomalies traditionally treated as congenital structural lesions with embolization or surgical resection. However, recurrence after embolization alone exceeds 90%, underscoring incomplete understanding of their biology. Next-generation sequencing has identified somatic gain-of-function mutations in KRAS, MAP2K1, BRAF, and HRAS as drivers of sporadic AVMs. Confined to lesional endothelial cells, these mutations activate RAS/MAPK signaling and support a neoplastic-like rather than purely structural disease model. This has therapeutic implications: MEK inhibitors, particularly trametinib, have shown angiographic and clinical responses in refractory AVMs. Interventional radiologists can obtain draining vein liquid-biopsy samples during embolization for molecular genotyping; embolization may increase circulating cell-free DNA yield. This review summarizes the molecular rationale, targeted therapy evidence, and integration of genotyping into embolization workflows to enable combined treatment strategies.Level of Evidence: No level of evidence assigned (Review Article).

#### [A] Inflammation and thrombosis: from pathophysiology to future treatments.

- PMID: 42955694
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Internal and emergency medicine
- 作者: Santagata D, Giovenali L, Guerrieri E, Falsetti L, Squizzato A, Moroncini G
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.1007/s11739-026-04574-w
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42955694/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Inflammation and coagulation are tightly interconnected host-defense systems that, when dysregulated, promote pathological thrombosis. This narrative review examines mechanisms, clinical settings, and therapeutic implications of thromboinflammation (TI) across venous, arterial, and microvascular thrombosis, with particular attention to the differences in clinical translation among vascular territories. Endothelial activation, tissue factor expression, platelet-leukocyte interactions, neutrophil extracellular traps, complement activation, inflammasome signalling, and impaired fibrinolysis converge to generate a procoagulant phenotype. These mechanisms modify Virchow's triad and contribute to venous, arterial, and microvascular thrombosis, although their relative importance varies across vascular territories. Clinically, TI is relevant in inflammatory bowel disease, rheumatologic and other immune-mediated disorders, sepsis, coronavirus disease 2019, clonal haematopoiesis, complement-mediated diseases, and atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease. TI offers a unifying framework for mechanistic risk assessment and therapeutic development. In atherothrombosis, randomised trials have demonstrated that selective modulation of inflammatory pathways, particularly the NLRP3-interleukin-1β-interleukin-6 axis and colchicine-sensitive pathways, can reduce recurrent cardiovascular events. By contrast, the role of inflammation-targeted therapies for primary or secondary prevention of VTE remains uncertain. Future studies should identify disease-specific biomarkers and test targeted anti-inflammatory or immunothrombotic strategies, alone or alongside anticoagulation, to prevent thrombosis without compromising haemostasis effectively.

#### [A] Integrated Network Pharmacology and Experimental Validation Implicate AKT1-Associated Signaling in the Protective Effects of Panax notoginseng Against Pulmonary Arterial Hypertension.

- PMID: 42959756
- Domain: Cross-domain vascular biology
- Domain 依据: Transferable mechanism appears with explicit vascular/endothelial context
- 转化相关性: Moderate
- 转化相关性依据: Different disease with a highly transferable vascular/endothelial mechanism
- 优先级依据: A: highly transferable cross-domain vascular mechanism
- 主题: 神经免疫/炎症治疗
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 期刊: Pulmonary circulation
- 作者: Sheng Y, Lu Z, Wang T, Tang H, Liu X, Zhao Z, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.1002/pul2.70435
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42959756/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

Pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH) is a progressive and fatal cardiopulmonary disease characterized by pulmonary vascular remodeling, endothelial dysfunction, inflammation, and increased pulmonary vascular resistance. Despite advances in targeted therapies, effective treatment options remain limited. This study investigated the molecular mechanisms underlying the therapeutic effects of Panax notoginseng against PAH through integrated computational and experimental validation. Network pharmacology was employed to identify the bioactive compounds and therapeutic targets of Panax notoginseng. Functional enrichment, protein-protein interaction network analysis, transcriptomic validation, molecular docking, surface plasmon resonance (SPR), molecular dynamics simulation, and MM/PBSA analyses were performed to characterize representative target-compound interactions. Experimental validation to predict the therapeutic mechanism was performed using RAW264.7 macrophages, pulmonary artery smooth muscle cells (PASMCs), and a Sugen5416/hypoxia-induced murine model of PAH. A total of 67 overlapping therapeutic targets were identified, with enrichment analyses highlighting inflammation, oxidative stress, apoptosis, vascular remodeling, and the PI3K/AKT signaling pathway. AKT1 was identified as a central hub gene and was significantly upregulated under hypoxic conditions. Quercetin and Liquiritigenin (DFV)exhibited stable interactions with AKT1, with quercetin demonstrating stronger binding affinity (-11.10 kcal/mol) than liquiritigenin (-9.33 kcal/mol). Consistent with these computational predictions, Panax notoginseng reduced AKT1 expression and phosphorylation, attenuated oxidative stress and inflammatory cytokine production, inhibited PASMC proliferation and migration, and significantly improved pulmonary hemodynamics, right ventricular hypertrophy, and pulmonary vascular remodeling in vivo. Collectively, these findings identify AKT1 as a key molecular target through which Panax notoginseng exerts therapeutic effects against PAH in vivo.

### [A] One trigger, two systems: post-infectious severe Guillain-Barre syndrome with concurrent central nervous system demyelination": a case report.

- PMID: 42928892
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Oxford medical case reports
- 作者: Numan M, Alhatou M, Hussain Z, Hussain M, Alhatou A, Hamad W
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.1093/omcr/omag202
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42928892/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Combined central and peripheral demyelination is a rare but serious neurological condition that is associated with poor outcomes. We report a 31-year-old male presenting with rapidly progressive Acute inflammatory demyelinating polyradiculoneuropathy (AIDP) variant of Guillain-Barré syndrome following an upper respiratory tract infection. The course was complicated by respiratory failure requiring intubation and significant autonomic dysfunction. Despite treatment with intravenous immunoglobulin, there was minimal clinical improvement. Further imaging of the central nervous system revealed multiple acute demyelinating lesions involving the brain and spinal cord. The patient subsequently received plasmapheresis and high-dose intravenous steroids without significant neurological recovery. A severe hospital-acquired infection delayed rituximab initiation until four months after symptom onset; however, neurological recovery remained minimal. This case highlights the rare coexistence of post-infectious central and peripheral demyelination. Early recognition of central involvement in patients with poor response to standard GBS therapy may help guide escalation of immunotherapy and potentially improve outcomes.

### [A] Synergistic Activity of Venetoclax and Rituximab in Multiply Relapsed or Refractory Waldenström Macroglobulinaemia.

- DOI: 10.1002/jha2.70435
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42920903/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: There are limited options for multiply relapsed/refractory (R/R) Waldenström Macroglobulinaemia (WM) post Bruton tyrosine kinase inhibitors (BTKi). The synergy of venetoclax and rituximab (Ven-R) may improve the poor outcomes with venetoclax monotherapy.
METHODS: A retrospective analysis was conducted of four patients treated with Ven-R.
RESULTS: All patients were refractory to covalent BTKi and had received 4-7 prior therapies. Three patients achieved a very good partial response. Treatment was lifesaving in one, facilitating reduction in plasmapheresis and improved cytopenias.
CONCLUSION: We demonstrate meaningful responses to Ven-R in R/R WM, supporting a rationale for prospective clinical trials of this combination.
TRIAL REGISTRATION: The authors have confirmed clinical trial registration is not needed for this submission.

### [A] Chronic Thromboembolic Pulmonary Hypertension With Cold Agglutinins Needing Pulmonary Thromboendarterectomy: The Use of Therapeutic Plasma Exchange.

- PMID: 42924162
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance

- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Pulmonary circulation
- 作者: Naqvi B, Bautista MA, Sanchez AP, Yang JZ, Fernandes TM, Poch DS, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.1002/pul2.70423
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42924162/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Cold agglutinins can be detected in patients with chronic thromboembolic pulmonary hypertension (CTEPH) during pulmonary thromboendarterectomy (PTE) pre-operative testing. Peri-operative management of these patients in anticipation of deep hypothermic circulatory arrest can be challenging and has not been standardized. We describe our relevant experience and suggest a potential protocolized pre-operative approach with this observational, retrospective cohort study. A chart review of all patients undergoing PTE surgery for CTEPH (1107 patients) at UC San Diego between 2019 and 2025 was performed. An Institutional Review Board approved quality improvement database on all patients undergoing PTE surgery was used. Twenty-one patients (1.9%) screened positive for cold agglutinins pre-PTE. A high co-prevalence of antiphospholipid syndrome and cold agglutinins was noted (17/21; 81%). Thermal amplitude testing (when possible) was positive for nine patients between 15°C and 18°C. Fifteen patients underwent therapeutic plasma exchange (median of two sessions) with albumin and plasma replacement fluid followed by PTE surgery with standard cooling protocol. There were no incidents of agglutination or hemolysis peri- or post-operatively. Three patients had post-operative airway hemorrhage, two of which developed heparin-induced thrombocytopenia (HIT). There was one mortality in the setting of HIT with recurrent thrombosis and bleeding. Median intensive care unit and hospital length of stays were 3 and 12.5 days, respectively. We suggest a protocolized approach for the identification and management of high-risk patients with cold agglutinins, which may include therapeutic plasma exchange pre-operatively. This can successfully mitigate the potential risk of hypothermic circulatory arrest causing agglutination during surgery.

### [A] Treatment of myelin oligodendrocyte glycoprotein antibody-associated disease (MOGAD): acute attacks, relapse prevention, and symptomatic care.

- PMID: 42921072
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in neurology
- 作者: Mahler JV, Vilardo M, Silva GD, Vishnevetsky A, Bilodeau PA, Levy M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fneur.2026.1965135
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42921072/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Myelin oligodendrocyte glycoprotein antibody-associated disease (MOGAD) is a distinct inflammatory demyelinating disorder of the central nervous system, and its diagnosis is guided by the 2023 International MOGAD Panel criteria. Its clinical spectrum includes optic neuritis, transverse myelitis, acute disseminated encephalomyelitis, and brainstem or cortical encephalitis, with a phenotype distribution that is strongly age-dependent. Although attacks are typically steroid-responsive and recovery is often good, roughly half of adults relapse, and it is the accumulation of incompletely recovered attacks, rather than a progressive phase, that drives long-term disability. Management therefore has three aims: to abort and reverse the acute attack, to prevent relapses in patients at risk, and to relieve residual symptomatic disability. This review synthesizes current evidence for acute treatment (high-dose corticosteroids, therapeutic plasma exchange, and intravenous immunoglobulin), the corticosteroid taper, and maintenance immunotherapy, with attention to when treatment should be started and when it may reasonably be stopped. We summarize comparative data favoring maintenance intravenous immunoglobulin, the emerging evidence for interleukin-6 receptor blockade, the more variable performance of B-cell depletion relative to AQP4-IgG-seropositive neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorder, and the first phase 3 trials in the field (satralizumab and rozanolixizumab). We give particular attention to children, in whom acute disseminated encephalomyelitis predominates and in whom cognitive, academic, growth and skeletal outcomes must shape treatment decisions, and to the real-world availability of antibody testing and therapy outside high-income settings. Finally, we address symptomatic care: visual rehabilitation, neuropathic and spasticity-related pain, fatigue, bladder dysfunction, cognition, and mood.

### [A] Neurexin-3α IgG-mediated autoimmune encephalitis with psychiatric manifestations and choreiform movements: a case report and literature review.

- PMID: 42922945
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in immunology
- 作者: Shen HY, Chen X, Yu RZ, Wu WJ, Dou XY, Fan XJ
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1944895
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42922945/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVE: To summarize the clinical manifestations, ancillary examination findings, treatment course, and prognosis of a patient with neurexin-3α IgG-mediated autoimmune encephalitis presenting with prominent psychiatric disturbances and choreiform movements, and to review the literature on the clinical spectrum of this disease.
CASE SUMMARY: A 52-year-old woman with a history of hypertension and biopsy-proven podocytopathy with a "full-house" immunofluorescence pattern showing diffuse positivity for IgG, IgA, IgM, C3, and C1q, diagnosed in February 2026; however, a definitive diagnosis of lupus podocytopathy remained uncertain, as the patient lacked systemic lupus erythematosus manifestations. She was admitted on April 3, 2026, with a 5-day history of psychiatric disturbances and involuntary choreiform movements in all four limbs, preceded by significant psychological stress (bereavement). Neurological examination revealed slowed responses and impaired calculation. Cranial MRI showed only bilateral periventricular small ischemic foci (Fazekas grade 1) with normal SWI. Initial EEG demonstrated sharp waves and spike-and-wave complexes in the left parietal, occipital, and temporal regions. CSF analysis revealed leukocytosis (21/μl, 90% mononuclear cells), protein of 0.4 g/L, elevated IgG index of 0.76, and elevated 24-hour intrathecal IgG synthesis rate of 5.63 mg/dl. CSF mNGS detected 453 EBV sequences (RPM) with a relative abundance of 97.48%. Serum EBV VCA-IgG and EBNA-IgG were elevated with negative IgM. Autoantibody testing followed a stepwise approach: initial screening for conventional autoimmune encephalitis, paraneoplastic, and demyelinating antibodies was negative; subsequent tissue-based assay (TBA) of CSF revealed strong positive immunoreactivity, prompting expanded antibody panel testing, which ultimately detected anti-neurexin-3α antibodies in serum (titer 1:30) and CSF (titer 1:100), confirmed by both cell-based assay (CBA) and TBA. The patient's condition progressively deteriorated, requiring endotracheal intubation on April 21 and tracheostomy on April 30. She received methylprednisolone pulse therapy at 500 mg/day for 5 days followed by a stepwise taper, intravenous immunoglobulin at 0.4 g/kg for 5 days, and five sessions of plasma exchange from April 30 to May 8 due to poor initial response. Choreiform movements gradually resolved after plasma exchange. She was weaned from mechanical ventilation on May 19 and decannulated on May 27. During the ICU course, she developed pulmonary infection with carbapenem-resistant Acinetobacter baumannii, which was controlled after antibiotic adjustment. Repeat EEG normalized on June 1. She was discharged from rehabilitation on June 9 on oral methylprednisolone 32 mg/day. On June 26, 2026, follow-up serum TBA showed weakly positive immunoreactivity, with the serum antibody titer remaining at 1:30. She subsequently received four infusions of efgartigimod at 400 mg each for consolidation immunotherapy and was transitioned to ofatumumab for long-term maintenance. At follow-up on August 6, 2026, the patient had no residual psychiatric symptoms or choreiform movements, with muscle strength recovered to 4+ in all limbs, full independence in daily activities, ambulation without assistance, and only mild gait instability.
CONCLUSION: Neurexin-3α IgG-mediated autoimmune encephalitis is a rare but treatable condition. Choreiform movements expand its clinical spectrum. The coexistence of podocytopathy raises a hypothesis of shared antigenicity between renal and neuronal neurexin family members, though this remains purely speculative. CSF EBV reactivation may represent a bystander phenomenon or epiphenomenon of severe immune dysregulation rather than a confirmed trigger. Tissue-based assay provides crucial diagnostic direction when conventional screening is negative. Early recognition, comprehensive immunotherapy, and meticulous ICU management are important determinants of favorable outcomes. Given the inherent limitations of a single case report, these findings warrant confirmation through larger cohort studies.

### [A] Fulminant serogroup B meningococcemia in an adolescent vaccinated against serogroups A and C: highlighting the serogroup B vaccination gap in China-a case report and literature review.

- PMID: 42923406
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Frontiers in medicine
- 作者: Hu Q, Jiang J, Zhu H, Yao Z, Tang S, Li H, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.3389/fmed.2026.1818444
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42923406/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Invasive meningococcal disease (IMD) remains an acute clinical challenge associated with high mortality and permanent disability. Although routine immunization has successfully suppressed historical lineages, an expanding epidemiological shift toward serogroup B in specific regions has exposed a population vulnerability, given that protein-based serogroup B vaccines are excluded from China's National Immunization Program (NIP, historically referred to as EPI). We present a case that characterizes the clinical trajectory of this vaccination mismatch.
CASE PRESENTATION: We report a case of a 12-year-old boy from Guangdong Province, China, who had previously received vaccination against Neisseria meningitidis serogroups A and C. He presented with fever and a rapidly progressive purpuric rash. Within hours, his condition deteriorated to septic shock, disseminated intravascular coagulation (DIC), and multiple organ dysfunction syndrome (MODS), consistent with purpura fulminans. Blood cultures confirmed N. meningitidis serogroup B. Following aggressive intensive care support-including antibiotics, vasopressors, mechanical ventilation, plasma exchange, and continuous renal replacement therapy-the patient survived, exhibiting complex ischemic sequelae that secondary cutaneous necrosis necessitated multi-stage surgical autologous grafting, which subsequently resulted in long-term functional extremity deficits.
CONCLUSION: This case underscores the hyperacute progression of serogroup B meningococcemia in an adolescent previously immunized against serogroups A and C. The clinical course illustrates an individual vulnerability under the current polysaccharide-based framework and highlights the public health relevance of reassessing regional vaccine strategies and enhancing surveillance for emerging serogroups.

### [A] Subacute immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura: prolonged prodromes and delayed diagnosis in 5 cases.

- PMID: 42921460
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Research and practice in thrombosis and haemostasis
- 作者: Tehrani TG, Sutton DJ, Whitmill R, Aquil M, Shenouda A, Razzak A, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct
- DOI: 10.1016/j.rpth.2026.106972
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42921460/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Immune thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (iTTP) is caused by a deficiency in ADAMTS-13 activity and is classically an acute-onset disorder. A subset of patients, however, may experience a more insidious course. Comprehensive reports of these presentations are sparse, and delayed diagnosis has been associated with increased thrombotic complications and mortality.
OBJECTIVES: To report on 5 cases of subacute iTTP presentations involving a prolonged prodromal period of nonspecific symptoms with gradual decline in platelet counts before acute deterioration and subsequent diagnosis.
METHODS: This retrospective case series identified delayed iTTP diagnoses in 5 patients with thrombocytopenia (platelets <150 ×109/L) and ADAMTS-13 activity <10 IU/dL managed at a single UK specialist tertiary referral center between 2022 and 2024. Clinical data, laboratory results, treatments, and outcomes were obtained from medical records.
RESULTS: The median age was 52 years (range, 3-78), and 3 patients were female. Median time from first nonspecific symptom to diagnosis was 84 days (range, 60-120), compared with 22 days (range, 5-65) from the first documented evidence of microangiopathic hemolytic anemia or thrombotic microangiopathy. All patients were found to have severe thrombocytopenia and ADAMTS-13 activity <10 IU/dL, with 3 showing anti-ADAMTS-13 IgG positivity. All patients were treated with plasma exchange, corticosteroids, rituximab, and caplacizumab; 3 required obinutuzumab for relapsed/refractory low ADAMTS-13 levels.
CONCLUSION: Insidious changes to blood count trends and prodromal symptoms risk being overlooked, delaying treatment and increasing morbidity and mortality. Broader awareness of subacute iTTP presentations outside hematology may improve timely diagnosis and management.

### [A] Toxic Epidermal Necrolysis Secondary to Nodal T-Follicular Helper Cell Lymphoma, not Otherwise Specified: A Case Report.

- PMID: 42923956
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1159/cde/abvag018
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42923956/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Nodal T-follicular helper cell lymphoma (nTFHL) is a rare subtype of peripheral T-cell lymphoma in which cutaneous manifestations occur in approximately half the patients.
CASE PRESENTATION: We report herein a case of toxic epidermal necrolysis (TEN) in a 62-year-old, male, Japanese patient with nTFHL, not otherwise specified. The patient experienced the development of rapidly progressive, erythematous plaques with blistering and erosion involving more than 30% of the body surface area. Histopathological examination of the skin revealed total epidermal necrosis without lymphoma cell infiltration while immunohistochemical analysis demonstrated depletion of FOXP3-positive cells. The TEN was successfully treated with systemic corticosteroid therapy, plasma exchange, and intravenous immunoglobulin. Although EBER-positive B cells in lymph nodes and EBV DNAemia were detected, their contribution to the TEN remains unclear.
CONCLUSION: This case expands the spectrum of severe, cutaneous manifestations associated with nTFHL and suggests that a reduction of regulatory T cells, previously reported in lymph nodes, may also occur locally in the skin and contribute to the development of cutaneous manifestations.

### [A] Longitudinally Extensive Hemorrhagic Myelitis in a Young Adult With Newly Diagnosed Duodenal Adenocarcinoma.

- PMID: 42923988

- 作者: Rahmathullah I, Peiris J, Hordagoda H, Godevithanage S, Weerasinghe N
- 发表日期: 2026 Sep
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.116743
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42923988/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Longitudinally extensive hemorrhagic myelitis is a rare clinical presentation with a broad differential diagnosis. We report a 20-year-old woman who presented with acute flaccid paraplegia, urinary retention, and a T2 sensory level. Magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated longitudinally extensive intramedullary spinal cord involvement with hemorrhagic features extending from C4 to the conus medullaris. Cerebrospinal fluid showed inflammatory changes and xanthochromia. Aquaporin-4 and myelin oligodendrocyte glycoprotein antibodies, oligoclonal bands, and tumor markers were negative. Further evaluation revealed a circumferential lesion in the second part of the duodenum, with histology confirming adenocarcinoma. No metastatic disease was identified. She received corticosteroids, plasma exchange, and intravenous immunoglobulin, followed by extensive surgical resection of the tumor. Neurological recovery remained poor. In the absence of another clear cause, the clinical and radiological findings raised the possibility of a paraneoplastic inflammatory myelopathy.

### [A] Murine Typhus Masquerading as Thrombotic Thrombocytopenic Purpura in a Patient With Newly Diagnosed HIV.

- PMID: 42923003
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Cureus
- 作者: Abdul Cader J, Lee SM, Tsai A
- 发表日期: 2026 Sep
- DOI: 10.7759/cureus.116679
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42923003/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-27

#### 摘要

Murine typhus is a flea-borne rickettsial disease caused by Rickettsia typhi. It presents with nonspecific symptoms and laboratory derangements, which can lead to a delay in diagnosis and treatment. We report the case of a 58-year-old man who presented with fever, altered mental status, nausea, vomiting, and diarrhea. Laboratory testing revealed thrombocytopenia, acute renal failure requiring emergent hemodialysis, transaminitis, and hyperbilirubinemia. An intermediate PLASMIC score initially prompted a concern for thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura, and urgent plasma exchange was initiated after the first round of dialysis, but this was ruled out when ADAMTS13 (a disintegrin and metalloproteinase with a thrombospondin type 1 motif, member 13) activity was low. During hospitalization, he was newly diagnosed with HIV, with a CD4 count of 65 cells/mm3. Given his fever, he was started on empiric doxycycline for presumed rickettsial infection in the setting of his symptoms, immunocompromised state, and lab abnormalities while confirmatory testing was pending. He completed a 10-day course of doxycycline, was started on antiretroviral therapy, and was discharged after recovery of renal function and resolution of thrombocytopenia, transaminitis, and changes in mental status. This case demonstrates that severe murine typhus can cause multi-organ failure in patients with newly diagnosed HIV/AIDS. Prompt empirical doxycycline, initiated before serologic confirmation, is essential to prevent progression to critical illness.

### [A] Daratumumab in immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura: emerging evidence for plasma cell-targeted therapy.

- PMID: 42954430
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Hematology (Amsterdam, Netherlands)
- 作者: Mohammed Saleh MF, Alzaidy N, Nassani M, Abdrabou AK, Nasiri A, Alzahrani H
- 发表日期: 2026 Dec 31
- DOI: 10.1080/16078454.2026.2753240
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42954430/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

INTRODUCTION: Immune mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura is a life threatening autoimmune thrombotic microangiopathy caused by severe ADAMTS13 deficiency due to inhibitory autoantibodies. Despite plasma exchange, corticosteroids, rituximab, and caplacizumab, some patients develop refractory disease or persistent and relapsing ADAMTS13 deficiency.
OBJECTIVES: To summarize the mechanistic rationale, clinical efficacy, safety, and potential therapeutic role of daratumumab in refractory or frequently relapsing immune mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura.
METHODS: A structured narrative review was conducted across five major bibliographic databases, PubMed, EMBASE, the Cochrane Library, China National Knowledge Infrastructure (CNKI), and Web of Science, from database inception through September 13, 2026. Searches of major hematology journal databases were also performed. Case reports, case series, registry analyses, multicenter cohort studies, and conference abstracts describing the use of daratumumab in acquired immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (iTTP) were included. Clinical setting, treatment regimen, ADAMTS13 response, time to response, relapse, and adverse events were extracted and qualitatively synthesized. Because reports differ in populations, endpoints, concomitant therapy, and possible patient overlap, findings were synthesized qualitatively without a pooled response estimate.
RESULTS: Six key clinical reports involving more than 40 patients were identified. Most patients had received multiple previous therapies, including plasma exchange, corticosteroids, rituximab, and caplacizumab. Daratumumab was administered intravenously at 16 mg/kg or subcutaneously at 1,800 mg, usually weekly for two to six doses. Across the available reports, ADAMTS13 recovery was observed in a substantial proportion of treated patients, although reported response rates varied according to the populations studied and the thresholds used to define response. Recovery commonly occurred within several weeks. Multicenter reports also described sustained remission in a proportion of responders, although biochemical relapse remained frequent during follow-up. Adverse events were generally manageable, with predominantly mild infusion reactions and infrequent serious infections. Other individualized treatment strategies described after rituximab failure or intolerance include bortezomib, optimized or alternative anti-CD20 therapy, ciclosporin, azathioprine, and mycophenolate.
CONCLUSION: Daratumumab is a promising plasma cell directed therapy for rituximab refractory or frequently relapsing immune mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura. Prospective studies are required to define its role within treatment algorithms.

### [A] Serum Profiles of Pembrolizumab, Cardiac Troponin T, and Interleukin-6 in a Plasma Exchange-Treated Patient With Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor-Related Myocarditis.

- PMID: 42956192
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Therapeutic drug monitoring
- 作者: Narita K, Suzuki S, Kuwahara K, Naito T
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 29
- DOI: 10.1097/FTD.0000000000001546
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42956192/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: A 77-year-old female patient with renal cell carcinoma, treated with intravenous pembrolizumab, developed pembrolizumab-related myocarditis accompanied by myasthenia gravis. For myasthenia gravis-related symptoms, intravenous methylprednisolone was administered on days 24-26 and 29-31 after the administration of 200 mg of pembrolizumab, and plasma exchange (PE) therapy was conducted on days 24, 26, 28, and 30.
METHODS: Serum pembrolizumab levels were monitored in conjunction with serum markers of myocarditis and interleukin-6 (IL-6) in the present patient treated with PE.
RESULTS: An elevated serum cardiac troponin T (cTnT) level of 0.542 ng/mL was observed 24 days after the first administration, and the serum pembrolizumab level was 15.7 µg/mL. After the first PE session, the serum levels of cTnT and IL-6 on day 25 were 0.406 ng/mL and 27.9 pg/mL, respectively, in conjunction with a decline of the serum pembrolizumab level to 4.83 µg/mL. Although the patient had a decreased serum pembrolizumab level of 3.86 µg/mL on day 29, the serum cTnT and IL-6 levels had increased to 0.843 ng/mL and 40.6 pg/mL, respectively. After the fourth PE session, serum levels of pembrolizumab, cTnT, and IL-6 on day 31 decreased to 3.62 µg/mL, 0.381 ng/mL, and 10.1 pg/mL, respectively. The overall removal rate after 4 sessions of PE was 76.9%, after which myasthenia gravis-related symptoms gradually disappeared.
CONCLUSIONS: This patient with a serum pembrolizumab level within the previously reported range developed myocarditis accompanied by myasthenia gravis. PE therapy efficiently extracted circulating pembrolizumab and IL-6 in this patient with immune checkpoint inhibitor-related myocarditis.

### [A] Immunosuppressive management of ANCA-associated glomerulonephritis in children.

- DOI: 10.1007/s00467-026-07568-0

#### 摘要

Anti-neutrophil cytoplasmic antibody (ANCA)-associated glomerulonephritis (AAGN) is a rare phenomenon in children and is associated with a significant risk of kidney failure. Over the last decade, there have been updates to clinical guidelines for the immunosuppressive management of this condition, but these continue to be based predominantly on clinical trial data in adults. Recent clinical trials have resulted in increasing use of rituximab for both maintenance and induction treatment, with renewed focus on the role of plasma exchange as part of induction treatment which remains controversial. Newer complement-targeting agents may provide an effective but more targeted and less toxic treatment strategy. This review focuses on immunosuppressive strategies for induction and maintenance treatment, considering recommendations from recent clinical guidelines and how these may be adapted for children and young people with AAGN.

### [A] Commutability assessment of candidate reference materials for total bilirubin assays in neonates.

- PMID: 42949072
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Clinical chemistry and laboratory medicine
- 作者: van der Hagen EAE, Oostendorp M, Bioch MM, Delatour V, Lo SF, Grote-Koska D, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 29
- DOI: 10.1515/cclm-2026-1261
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42949072/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

OBJECTIVES: Neonatal total bilirubin (TBIL) measurements are the cornerstone for treatment decisions in neonatal hyperbilirubinemia. However, neonatal TBIL assays suffer from substantial inter-assay differences and standardization is hampered by the lack of commutable secondary reference materials. Therefore, the aim of this study was to assess commutability of candidate reference materials (RMs) to support standardization efforts.
METHODS: Three types of materials were investigated as candidate RMs: bilirubin-spiked serum materials prepared by the Dutch EQA provider SKML, residual material from neonatal exchange transfusions, and NIST SRM916b material dissolved in adult serum. Commutability of these materials was assessed using the prediction interval and difference in bias approaches. To this end, all candidate RMs and clinical samples were measured on ten different routine methods and compared to the reference measurement procedure.
RESULTS: For three methods, commutability could not be assessed due to too high non-selectivity. The exchange transfusion material demonstrated the highest degree of commutability, the highest TBIL concentration being commutable for all methods. Also, the two SRM-spiked samples with the highest TBIL concentrations showed to be commutable for six out of seven selective methods, except for the Ortho Vitros XT3400 platform.
CONCLUSIONS: Exchange transfusion materials provide a robust neonatal serum-based material which can be used as secondary reference material and as EQA material to support and monitor future efforts to improve standardization of neonatal TBIL assays.

### [A] Adjunctive therapeutic plasma exchange as rescue therapy in septic shock due to streptococcal infection: a multicentric retrospective cohort study.

- PMID: 42955503
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 今日新文献
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Intensive care medicine experimental
- 作者: Ruwisch J, Buhlmann A, Wild L, Wendel-Garcia PD, Pape T, Hofmaenner DA, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 28
- DOI: 10.1186/s40635-026-00984-4
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42955503/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Septic shock due to streptococcal infection (SRSS) carries a high mortality and is in part driven by toxin-mediated inflammation. Therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE) may modulate the septic host responses by removing injurious mediators while replenishing protective plasma factors, but data specific to SRSS is lacking. We performed a retrospective, multicenter, cohort study of adults with SRSS between 2012-2024.
METHODS: Patients from three tertiary care ECMO centers in Germany and Switzerland were included requiring (i) streptococcal spp. detected in blood or primary focus and (ii) septic shock according to sepsis-3 definition. Adjunctive TPE with fresh frozen plasma at the intensivists` discretion was compared with standard of care (SOC). The primary outcome was 90-day-hospital mortality. Key exploratory outcomes included 24-h kinetics of vasoactive-inotropic score(VIS) and lactate, as well as SRSS-related complications. Results were validated in a 1:1 propensity score-matched (PSM) cohort.
RESULTS: Of 149 patients, 31 (20.8%) received TPE and 118 (79.2%) SOC. TPE patients were more severely ill at baseline: SOFA 14 (IQR 13.5-15) vs 11 (IQR 8-14) (p<0.001); lactate 6.4 vs 2.8 mmol/L (p<0.001); Vasoactive-Inotropic-Score (VIS) 70.7 vs 23.3 (p<0.001); more frequent invasive ventilation (87% vs 65%), VV-ECMO (36% vs 19%), and renal replacement therapy (96.8% vs 52.5%). Overall, 90-day hospital mortality was 27.5% (TPE 29.0%, SOC 27.1%). Following SOFA-score adjustment, TPE was associated with lower mortality risk (aHR 0.67 [95% CI 0.5-0.9]; p=0.009). This was validated in the PSM cohort, with an aHR for TPE of 0.44 (95% CI 0.3-0.8); p=0.002. Mixed-effects modeling demonstrated a significant VIS reduction only with TPE (fixed effect 24-h change, p=0.027) and improved lactate decrease versus SOC.
CONCLUSION: In SRSS, adjunctive TPE was associated with rapid hemodynamic stabilization and improved lactate kinetics. Exploratory adjusted survival analyses support prospective evaluation of TPE in SRSS.

### [A] Efficacy of Fampridine on Refractory Optic Neuritis in Neuromyelitis Optica Spectrum Disorders.

- PMID: 42946677
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Annals of clinical and translational neurology
- 作者: Wang H, Chen W, Li X, Lu H, Li C, Zhang X, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 27
- DOI: 10.1002/acn3.70557
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42946677/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Neuromyelitis optica spectrum disorders (NMOSD) usually occur with acute refractory optic neuritis (ON). However, there is a lack of efficacious treatments to improve recovery. Since fampridine has been found to improve the prognosis of multiple sclerosis (MS) and MS-related ON, it is necessary to explore the efficacy of fampridine on visual functions in NMOSD-ON.
METHODS: Patients aged 14-80 who suffered from acute refractory AQP4-IgG positive ON within 2 weeks onset and with high-dose corticosteroid treatment were included. Patients treated with sustained-release fampridine (4-aminopyridine, 4-AP) as add-on treatment for 3 months were compared with patients without 4-AP (non-4-AP). Visual functional scores (VFS), Opticospinal Impairment Scale (OSIS), the logarithm of the minimum angle of resolution (LogMAR) of visual acuity (VA), the mean deviation (MD) value of the static visual field, and retina nerve fiber layer (RNFL) thickness were measured and followed up from baseline to 6 months.
RESULTS: Patients with 4-AP add-on treatment improved VFS or OSIS greater than that with control (non-4-AP) at 3 and 6 months compared with baseline (all p < 0.05). 4-AP add-on treatment also increased the proportion of better improvement of ON (VFS ≤ 2) at 3 months (36.4% vs. 7.1% in control, p < 0.05 adjusted for plasma exchange), and 4-AP improved visual field at 6 months (p < 0.05 adjusted for plasma exchange).
CONCLUSION: Fampridine as an add-on treatment has potential functional benefit on refractory NMOSD-ON and improves visual functions after the attack of ON.

#### 摘要

Atypical hemolytic uremic syndrome (aHUS) is a life-threatening thrombotic microangiopathy caused by complement dysregulation. Although anti-complement factor H (CFH) autoantibodies are a well-recognized cause of aHUS, their occurrence in elderly patients is rare, and optimal management in this population remains unclear. We report the case of an 85-year-old man who presented with acute kidney injury, thrombocytopenia, and microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, alongside severe hypertension. ADAMTS13 activity was preserved, and no secondary causes of thrombotic microangiopathy were identified, leading to a clinical suspicion of complement-mediated thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA). At the time of clinical decision-making, anti-CFH antibody status was not yet available;serum creatinine trends during hemodialysis could not reliably indicate intrinsic renal recovery, and hematological markers, although improving, did not clearly indicate complete resolution of hemolysis. Following an individualized risk assessment regarding plasma exchange (PEX) and immunosuppressive therapy in an octogenarian, ravulizumab was selected as the initial treatment due to its etiology-independent mechanism of action. Following initiation, hematological parameters continued to improve, and renal function gradually recovered, allowing discontinuation of hemodialysis. Subsequent testing revealed elevated anti-CFH antibody levels without pathogenic complement gene variants, confirming the diagnosis of anti-CFH antibody-associated aHUS. This case highlights that, in clinical settings with diagnostic uncertainty, ravulizumab monotherapy may be considered as an individualized initial approach in exceptional cases where PEX and immunosuppression are judged unsuitable based on clearly documented patient-specific risks, and requires careful follow-up including serial anti-CFH antibody monitoring and consideration of immunosuppression if antibody titers persist or relapse risk remains high.

### [A] Short-term outcomes, longer-term disability and relapse risk in LGI1-antibody encephalitis.

- PMID: 42934970
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Brain : a journal of neurology
- 作者: Ramanathan S, Soltys J, Michael S, Mackrill SV, Cleaver J, Williams R, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 27
- DOI: 10.1093/brain/awag370
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42934970/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Leucine-rich glioma-inactivated-1 antibody encephalitis (LGI1-Ab-E) is a common and immunotherapy-responsive form of encephalitis. However, it is not known which immunotherapy regimens improve short-term outcomes, longer-term disability and relapse risk. Further, it is not known if the widely-utilised modified Rankin Scale (mRS) accurately captures quality of life (QoL) in LGI1-Ab-E, complicating disability measurements and interpretation of immunotherapy responses. A multicentre, international, real-world comparative analysis of patients with LGI1-Ab-E. Retrospective data from patient records evaluated short-term seizure and cognitive outcomes, 1-6 year disability, and future relapse risk associations, by integrating demographics, clinical features investigations and exposures to immunotherapies and antiseizure medications (ASMs). Patient- and neurologist-rated QoL assessments were used to establish an evidence-based mRS threshold for defining clinically significant disability. Cox proportional hazard models, Anderson-Gill, and generalised linear mixed-effects models were employed. 181 patients were included, with median age at onset 63 years (range 23 - 92); 122/181 (67%) were male. Short-term outcomes, evaluated at both five and ten weeks post-treatment initiation, identified exposure to immunotherapies associated with seizure resolution (OR = 13.97, 95%CI = 6.10-32.01; p < 0.0001 and OR = 8.53, 95%CI = 4.14-17.55; p < 0.0001, respectively), notably with corticosteroids, plasma exchange and rituximab, but not ASMs, intravenous immunoglobulins or steroid sparing agents. Conversely, seizure recurrence was observed after discontinuation of oral prednisolone, but not other immunotherapies or ASMs (OR = 39.53, 95%CI = 12.70-122.20, p < 0.0001). To evaluate longer-term disability, first, mRS scores were calibrated using QoL assessments: this identified a significant increase in patient-rated disability at mRS score of 2. Hence, using mRS 0-1 as a favourable outcome, we identified worse mRS at nadir associated with greater one-year disability (OR = 2.56, 95%CI = 1.56-4.20, p = 0.0003), and each month of delayed immunotherapy associated with a 16% increased risk of disability (HR = 1.16, 95%CI = 1.06-1.35, p = 0.004). Additionally, absence of immunotherapy associated both with greater one-year disability (OR = 5.67; 95% CI 1.19-26.54, p = 0.03) and a relapsing course (OR = 7.64, 95%CI = 2.51-21.77, p = 0.0002). Both a relapsing course, observed in 38% of patients, and the number of relapses associated with step-wise worsening of disability and QoL-outcomes. Rituximab exposure was the only identified clinical factor associated with reduced future relapse risk, by 93% (HR = 0.07, 95%CI = 0.01-0.48, p = 0.002). Persistent serum LGI1-IgMs, but not LGI1-IgGs, at one-year, associated with future relapses (HR = 11.69, 95%CI = 1.58-145.80, p = 0.02). Distinctive clinical factors and select immunotherapies, particularly intravenous methylprednisolone and plasma exchange, but not ASMs, associated with favourable short-term outcomes, patient-centric longer-term disability and relapses in LGI1-Ab-E. Our findings provide evidence-based disability thresholds, highlight novel prognostic biomarkers, and pragmatically guide LGI1-Ab-E care, pending randomized clinical trials.

### [A] Embolization of Thyroid Arteries as a Bridge Therapy Before Thyroidectomy in a Patient With Thyrotoxicosis: Case Report.

- PMID: 42936566
- Domain: Out-of-scope

- 作者: Gomez-Fontalvo CA, Díaz-Jaimes SA, Rangel-Villamizar MP, Moreno-Diaz DA, Castellanos-Bueno R

- DOI: 10.1155/crie/8194736
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42936566/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Thyroid artery embolization (TAE) is a minimally invasive technique that induces selective ischemic necrosis of the thyroid parenchyma, thereby reducing thyroid hormone synthesis with few associated complications. Successful cases of TAE as a bridge therapy before total thyroidectomy in patients with refractory thyrotoxicosis are rare. We present the case of a 36-year-old man with a history of Graves' disease and poor adherence to medical therapy who presented with clinical features of thyrotoxicosis. On physical examination, he exhibited active moderate-to-severe orbitopathy (EUGOGO classification; clinical activity score 5/7) and a grade III goiter, reflecting advanced and poorly controlled disease. Paraclinical findings revealed markedly elevated free T4 levels and suppressed TSH. The diagnosis of uncontrolled hyperthyroidism was made, with a Burch-Wartofsky score of 40 points, indicating an impending thyroid storm. Medical therapy at maximally tolerated doses did not result in a complete clinical response; therefore, a total thyroidectomy was planned, preceded by therapeutic plasma exchange (TPE). Due to intolerance to TPE, TAE was performed as a bridge therapy, followed by total thyroidectomy without complications. The patient was discharged with a resolution of symptoms and progressive normalization of thyroid hormone levels. TAE may represent a valuable bridging therapy before total thyroidectomy in selected patients with severe thyrotoxicosis due to Graves' disease who are refractory to or unsuitable for conventional therapies.

### [A] Normal Early Investigations Do Not Exclude Bickerstaff Brainstem Encephalitis: Highlighting Diagnostic and Treatment Pitfalls.

- PMID: 42936526
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory

- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Case reports in neurological medicine
- 作者: Kmail H, Jaradat M, Abbas A, Abu Zaineh O, Kharouf A, AlShaer QN
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.1155/crnm/8222960
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42936526/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-30

#### 摘要

Bickerstaff brainstem encephalitis (BBE) is a rare immune-mediated disorder characterized by ophthalmoplegia, cerebellar ataxia, and impaired consciousness. It is now recognized as a variant of Guillain-Barré syndrome (GBS), sharing common pathogenic mechanisms with Miller Fisher syndrome. However, early diagnosis remains challenging, particularly when initial investigations are normal. We report a 20-year-old previously healthy woman presenting with rapidly progressive ophthalmoplegia, bulbar dysfunction, flaccid quadriparesis, areflexia, and impaired consciousness following a febrile upper respiratory illness. Early cerebrospinal fluid analysis and brain MRI were normal. Anti-GQ1b antibodies were positive, and nerve conduction studies showed isolated proximal conduction abnormalities with absent F-waves. The patient developed respiratory failure requiring mechanical ventilation and was treated with an extended course of plasma exchange, resulting in near-complete recovery. This case highlights a critical diagnostic pitfall: BBE, a variant of GBS, can present with a life-threatening brainstem syndrome despite entirely normal early investigations. We emphasize the value of recognizing the paradoxical combination of areflexia and extensor plantar responses and the use of F-wave studies as an early electrophysiological foothold when standard studies are unrevealing. Furthermore, it demonstrates that even in catastrophic, ventilator-dependent presentations, an extended course of plasma exchange can lead to a favorable outcome, suggesting a potential therapeutic strategy for severe presentations of BBE.

### [A] Prevalence, Predictors, and Management of Acute Chest Syndrome in Patients with Sickle Cell Disease at Muhimbili National Hospital, Tanzania: A Retrospective Study.

- PMID: 42953003
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Journal of blood medicine
- 作者: Masoud SA, Ally M, Kakumbula HT, Nasser A, Chamba C, Lyimo M, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026
- DOI: 10.2147/JBM.S603553
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42953003/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

BACKGROUND: Acute Chest Syndrome (ACS) is a potentially life-threatening complication of Sickle Cell Disease (SCD), contributing significantly to hospitalizations and mortality. While extensively studied in high-income settings, there is limited data on its prevalence, associated factors, and treatment patterns in Tanzania.
OBJECTIVE: To determine the prevalence, predictors, and management of ACS among SCD patients admitted at Muhimbili National Hospital (MNH), Dar es Salaam, between January 2019 and December 2024.
METHODS: A retrospective study was conducted using medical records of 1,257 SCD patients admitted to MNH. Data on demographics, clinical and laboratory characteristics, and treatment interventions were extracted and analyzed. Modified Poisson regression was used to identify factors associated with ACS.
RESULTS: The prevalence of ACS was 24.3%. ACS was more likely among children under the age of five years compared to adults above 17 years (adjusted prevalence ratio [aPR] = 2.04, 95% CI: 1.76-2.38), in patients with upper respiratory tract infections (aPR = 1.62, 95% CI: 1.47-1.79) and in those with vaso-occlusive crises (aPR = 2.24, 95% CI: 2.12-2.38), while the use of hydroxyurea (aPR = 0.78, 95% CI: 0.78-0.79) and a history of asthma (aPR = 0.37, 95% CI: 0.27-0.47) were associated with a lower prevalence of ACS. Treatment offered included antibiotics (94.4%), pain management (87.2%), intravenous hydration (76.7%), oxygen therapy (43.9%), simple top-up blood transfusion (42.0%) and mechanical ventilation (9.8%). Exchange transfusion was underutilized (0.7%).
CONCLUSION: ACS remains a common complication among patients with SCD admitted to MNH, particularly among younger patients and those presenting with upper respiratory tract infections or vaso-occlusive crises. Hydroxyurea use and a history of asthma were associated with a lower prevalence of ACS in this cohort. Exchange transfusion was the least utilized treatment modality despite current recommendations. These findings highlight the need to strengthen preventive strategies, improve access to hydroxyurea, and address barriers to advanced ACS management, including exchange transfusion.

### [A] Anti-GBM/ANCA double-positive crescentic nephritis in an elderly patient presenting with heart failure-like symptoms: A case report.

- PMID: 42937723
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
- 优先级依据: A: plasma exchange/plasmapheresis/blood exchange/immunoadsorption
- 主题: 换血/血浆置换疗法
- 来源类型: 近期补位
- 独立科研兴趣: Plasma exchange / blood exchange
- 期刊: Medicine
- 作者: Shi M, Shi G, Liu H, Huang Y, Luo N, Ge Y, et al.
- 发表日期: 2026 Oct 23
- DOI: 10.1097/MD.0000000000051225
- PubMed: https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42937723/
- 日报日期: 2026-10-31

#### 摘要

RATIONALE: Anti-glomerular basement membrane (anti-GBM) antibody disease and antineutrophil cytoplasmic antibody (ANCA)-associated vasculitis represent the 2 leading causes of rapidly progressive glomerulonephritis (RPGN). The coexistence of these conditions is rare, typically progresses rapidly, and has a poor prognosis. Elderly patients often present with nonspecific systemic symptoms, increasing the likelihood of misdiagnosis and treatment delay.
PATIENT CONCERNS: A 74-year-old woman presented with a one-month history of intermittent shortness of breath, worsening progressively over ten days, accompanied by nausea and vomiting. She denied hemoptysis, gross hematuria, rash, or arthralgia.
DIAGNOSES: Initial assessment suggested heart failure. However, laboratory results revealed severe renal impairment (creatinine: 1025.99 μmol/L). Renal biopsy demonstrated crescent formation in over 80% of glomeruli, with immunofluorescence showing linear immunoglobulin G deposits along glomerular capillary loops. Serological testing revealed markedly elevated anti-GBM antibody (>400 RU/mL) and MPO-ANCA (108.4 RU/mL) levels, together with a perinuclear ANCA (P-ANCA) pattern on indirect immunofluorescence, supporting the diagnosis of anti-GBM glomerulonephritis with concomitant MPO-ANCA positivity.
INTERVENTIONS: Treatment included methylprednisolone pulse therapy, cyclophosphamide, and 3 plasma exchange sessions. Additional supportive measures comprised hemodialysis, antihypertensive therapy, and management of renal anemia.
OUTCOMES: Although the patient's uremic symptoms improved markedly, renal function did not recover. She was discharged on hemodialysis and was subsequently transitioned to maintenance peritoneal dialysis. By January 2026, the anti-GBM antibody level had decreased markedly to 4.27 RU/mL, below the positive cutoff, and MPO-ANCA had also become negative. At the last follow-up in April 2026, the patient remained dialysis-dependent and experienced recurrent hospitalizations for pneumonia, heart failure, and acute pancreatitis.
LESSONS: This case emphasizes that elderly patients with anti-GBM/ANCA double-positive crescentic glomerulonephritis may initially present with nonspecific symptoms, increasing the risk of incorrect diagnosis. Although combination therapy resulted in symptomatic improvement and serological remission, renal function failed to recover and dialysis dependence persisted. Screening for anti-GBM antibody and ANCA should therefore be considered in patients with unexplained rapidly progressive deterioration of kidney function or active urinary sediment, and renal biopsy should be performed promptly when clinically indicated.

### [A] When Pain Crisis Turns Lethal: A Case Report of Fat Embolism Syndrome in Sickle Cell Disease.

#### 摘要

Fat embolism syndrome (FES) is a rare, life-threatening complication of sickle cell disease (SCD), often underrecognized due to overlap with acute chest syndrome, sepsis, and thrombotic microangiopathies. We report a 37-year-old man with sickle cell disease who presented with an acute vaso-occlusive crisis and rapidly deteriorated with respiratory failure, cardiovascular collapse, and neurological dysfunction. Common etiologies including pulmonary embolism, infection, and stroke were excluded. Laboratory evidence of hemolysis, cytopenias, elevated ferritin, and acute cor pulmonale suggested systemic FES secondary to bone marrow necrosis. The patient underwent urgent red cell exchange transfusion, followed by therapeutic plasma exchange and corticosteroids, resulting in significant clinical and hemodynamic recovery. This case highlights the importance of early recognition and aggressive multimodal therapy in SCD-FES.

#### 摘要

Immune-mediated thrombotic thrombocytopenic purpura (iTTP) results from severe ADAMTS13 deficiency and may relapse despite modern therapy, with survival exceeding 95% in contemporary cohorts. We report the case of a 43-year-old patient with multiple clinical and ADAMTS13 relapses over 15 years, who developed intolerance or had incomplete responses to several immunosuppressive agents, including anti-CD20 and anti-CD38 therapies. Splenectomy was considered high risk due to significant cardiac comorbidity. Off-label zanubrutinib, a second-generation Bruton tyrosine kinase (BTK) inhibitor licensed locally for chronic lymphocytic leukemia (CLL) was initiated after the sixth relapse, which was a clinical one, simultaneously with plasma exchange (PEX) and corticosteroids. Following initiation, ADAMTS13 activity rose, platelet counts normalized, and the patient has remained in clinical remission for 20 months under zanubrutinib. This case illustrates the feasibility of BTK inhibition in selected, treatment-limited iTTP.

### [A] Cryofibrinogenemia - an often-neglected rare disease: a case series.

- PMID: 42961161
- Domain: Out-of-scope
- Domain 依据: No strong CCM/vascular-malformation or transferable vascular mechanism evidence
- 转化相关性: Exploratory
- 转化相关性依据: Independent plasma exchange/blood exchange research interest; not disease-domain relevance
